1b1(rnying rgyud): @##//rgya gar skad du/de bI dzA li ma hA mA yA ta b+h+ya nA ma/bod skad du/lha mo sgyu 'phrul dra ba chen mo zhes bya ba'i rgyud/bcom ldan 'das gsang ba chen po 
1b2(rnying rgyud): la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis gsungs pa nyid du gyur pa'i tshe na bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa nam mkha' dang mnyam pa'i rnal 'byor chen po dang /rnal 
1b3(rnying rgyud): 'byor ma chen mo dang /shin tu mos pa chen mo dang /mos pa dang bcas pa srung bar byed pa/rab tu rnam par srung bar byed nye ba mo dang bcas pa nye bar zhi ba chen po dang zhi bar mdzad pa 
1b4(rnying rgyud): chen po mdzes pa dang mdog tu ldan pa/shes pa dang skyabs su ldan pa ni 'di lta ste/gtso bo rnal 'byor 'khor lo zhes bya ba dang /rnal 'byor chen po rdo rje nyid du 'dus pa zhes 
1b5(rnying rgyud): bya ba dang /rnal 'byor che na po btang snyoms zhes bya ba dang /rnal 'khor chen po dbang du mdzad pa zhes bya ba dang /rnal 'phyor shin tu rnam par rgya gram gyi 
2a1(rnying rgyud): @#//tshul 'dzin pa dang bcas pa ni cir yang ston pa'i gzugs kyis kha dog bzhi dang ldan te 'dug pa'i cha lugs kyis cod pan lnga dang ldan te/ma bsgribs mi sgrib bsgrib pa mi 'gog sgrib tu med pa'i dbyings nas yang dag pa'i gzhal 
2a2(rnying rgyud): yas chen po na mnyam pa chen po'i tshul rdo rjes sku ma bkag pas mdzad pa bsam gyis mi khyab pas bzhugs so//de yang 'di ltar so sor gsal bar mnyam pa chen po dang 'khor dang bcas pa mnyam pa chen pos yo byad chen po ni kun gyi gdung 'dzin pa'i rang 
2a3(rnying rgyud): bzhin du gnas pa ni brten pas phyin ci ma log pa dang gnyis su du ma med pa ni gzungs rnal 'byor ma chen mo ste/byang chub chen mo nam mkha' zhes bya ba dang /byang chub chen mo nam ma mkha' 'dzin ma zhes bya ba dang /byang chung chen mos srung ma zhes 
2a4(rnying rgyud): bya ba dang /byang chub che na mo rlan par byed pa chen mo dang /byang chub chen mo'i rnam par khengs pa spang ba zhes bya ba dang /byang cub chen mo las grub par mdzad ma zhes bya ba dang /byangs chub chen mo rdo rje rab tu chags pa chen po 'dzin zhes bya ba dang /rab 
2a5(rnying rgyud): tu ma chags pa gsod ces bya ba dang /byang chub sen mo rab tu rmongs sel zhes bya ba dang /byang chu chen mo gsung gi 'khor lo ma zhes bya ba ste/de dag kyang 'di lta ste/shin tu ut+pal lta bu'i mdog dang /sha ga kra dang /bai DUr+ya dang /
2a6(rnying rgyud): pad+ma rA ga dang /asma gar pa la sogs pa rdzas kyi bsngags pas 'phags par 'tsher ba ma smin pa med par bzhugs pa ni rnal 'byor ma kun tu bzang po 'khor dang bcas pa'o//de yang gus pa chen po gsang ba dang /gus pa chen po sna tshogs pa'i 
2b1(rnying rgyud): dri dang /gus pa chen po dbang sgyur ba ni gsang ba'i dbang po zhes bya ba dang /gsal ba 'dzin byed ma dang /rnam par dri yi gnas su gyur ba dang /rnam par sgra byed pa'i gzugs 'dzin pa'o//de nyid mthong bar byed pa'i dbang po dang /nyan par byed pa'i dbang 
2b2(rnying rgyud): po dang /bsung byed chen po'i dbang po dang /btar ba chen po'i dbang po dang /shin tu gtong bar mdzad pa dang /shin tu rtog par mdzad pa dang /shin du 'dzin dbang chen po dang /shin tu grub par mdzad pa dang /cha lugs mi gtong ba'i btsun mo dang bcas 
2b3(rnying rgyud): pa'o//yod pa rnam par rtog par mdzad pa dang /med pa sel bar mdzad pa dang /rnam par 'du shes rgyal ba yin par mdzad pa dang /shinatu 'gog pa rnam par 'joms pa chen mo dang /mngon spyod kyi las kyis gnyis su med par 'khril nas chos kyi dbyings 
2b4(rnying rgyud): mnyam pa chen po'i gzhal yas na bzhugs so//de nas bcom ldan 'das gsang ba chen mo'i rnal 'byor ma'i gzhal yas zhu dgyes nas ma skyes pa'i dbyings shes rab kyi tshogs chen mo su khyud kun dang ldan pa/zla rgyas lta bu'i dkyil 'khor mtha' dbus med par 
2b5(rnying rgyud): khyab pa/rin po che kun dang ldan pa/grwa dang ngos dang kha dog dang dbyibs rma khyon dpag tu med par gyur pa/ye shes bsam gyis mi khyab pa cha lugs kyis rnam par mdzes pa/yid bzhin chen po'i rgyan gyis rnam par spras pa/sna tshogs pa'i bye brag gis 
2b6(rnying rgyud): rgyal ba rnams kyi gdung 'dzin pa/sgyu 'phrul chen po'i sgo bzhi dang ldan pa/theg brgya'i rnal 'byor rnams kyi theg pa chen po 'bab gzhol dge sdig chen po'i mchog shin tu gsang ba chen mo dang bcas pa/pad+ma chen po'i dbus na lceb bdog bzhi'i bye brag gis 
2b7(rnying rgyud): mdzes par gyur pa/phyogs bzhi bre bar mi pa gsum gyi cha lugs kyis gsang ba chen po'i dkyil 'khor du gyur pa'o//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dkyil 'khor sgrib pa mchog tu sel ba ste le'u dang po'o////
3a1(rnying rgyud): @#//spyod chen po gdung ba mchog tu sel bar mdzad pa'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs te/'dzum pa chen pos rab tu chags pa chen po la dbang sgyur bar dgyes te/gsang ba chen mo'i mkha' la padam dbang gi mdog gis rgyas par mdzad pa 
3a2(rnying rgyud): oM su ra tA su khe zhes brjod pas rdo rje rigs bsam gyis mi khyab pa btsun mo dang bcas par gyur to//de nas rab tu 'di brjod par mdzad do//bzhi pa kha dog dkar la 'tsher//btang snyoms kun gyi spro bsdu yis//gsum pa rdzogs par gsal ba dang //
3a3(rnying rgyud): phyag rgya'i tshogs mchog de dag las//rgyal mo dag dang mnyam du dgyes//phyag rgya rdo rje dril bu 'dzin//bcu gsum pa yi cha lugs kyis//che chung med par gsal bar bsgom//dbu la gnyis pa'i yi ger ldan//gsal ba nyid kyi dus dag tu//cod pan 
3a4(rnying rgyud): lnga yi rin chen las//rgyan gyi rigs ni gsal ba yin//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi mchog tu gsang ba rgyan dam pa zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//gsum pa mthing 'bar yi ge che//rigs kyi rin chen spro bsdu las//phyag rgyar gsal bas skur gsal 
3a5(rnying rgyud): ba//rgyal mo dag dang mnyamadu dgyes//sgrib pa kun spangs sdig med sdoms//gnyis pa yi ni yi ge las//cod pan lnga ldan yi ger gsal//thams cad blo sbyangs yid bzhin gter//mnyam pa 'khril ba'i tshul 'dzin pa//rin chen rigs kyi phyag 
3a6(rnying rgyud): rgyar gsal//las thams cad kyi mchog rgyal lo rnam par rol pa'i phyag rgya'o//padam rA ga 'dra ba'i mdog/chos sku zla tshes rab ldan pa//gsal ba'i kha dog mdzes pa ni//thams cad ma lus dbang du mdzad//sbyar dang bskyed dang 
3a7(rnying rgyud): bsdu ba yis//phyag rgyar gsal bas dbang gi sku//btsun mo dag dang mnyam du dgyes//gnyis su med par 'khril ba yis//dbu ru yi ge gnyis pa ldan//rin chen rigs kyi spro bsdu las//cod pan rigs lnga kha dog ste//ji bzhin 
3b1(rnying rgyud): pa yi ting 'dzin gyis//phyag rgya bzhugs tshul pad+ma'i tshul//mnyam par bsam pas dkyil 'khor yin//mi 'byed pa yi ting 'dzin gyis//rdzogs pa'i dkyil 'khor kun 'dus mchog/pad+ma rigs kyi phyag rgyar gsal//mun pa sel ba'i mar me bzhin//
3b2(rnying rgyud): gsal ngag yid gnyis med par bsgom//chu skyes 'dzum pa'i 'od du ldan//steg dang sprod pa'i rol mo yis//dgyes shing mnyam pa'i mchod par bsham//'di ltar gsal bas dbang yang rdzogs//thams cad sdud pa zhes su bya//de bzhin dbang 
3b3(rnying rgyud): ste las thams cad kyi phyag rgya'i ye shes so//dus gsum pa yi mdzad pa che//rigs kun 'dus pa bde gshegs las//mnyam pa ji bzhin ting 'dzin te//yi ge 'bru gcig kun gyi mchog/bshad pas khyad par las kun rdzogs//chu skyes gsal 
3b4(rnying rgyud): ba'i 'dab ma steng //gnyis su med par gsal ba ni//rnam pa gnyis kyi bye brag gis//'dus pa gsal bas skur gyur pa//'dod cing chags pa ma yin zhing //blo la yid gnyis med pa dang //phyag rgyar gsal bas rgyal ba'i sku//rgyal mo dang ni 
3b5(rnying rgyud): mnyam du dgyes//dbu gnyis pa yi yi ger ldan//sbyin pa rgya chen khengs pa na//rang gi 'dod pa'i gnas su bsdu//bod tshod lo grangs khyad par med//gnyis su med par 'khril bar gsal//dus kun du ni mnyam ldan pas//gsang ba'i mchod 
3b6(rnying rgyud): pa rab tu rdzogs//zla rgyas lta bu'i gtso bo bsgom//chags pa med par 'dod sdud pa'i//yum dang lhan cig bsgom pa ni//A li kA li byed pa'i don//phyag rgya las kyi bye brag gis//bde ba chen po'i tshul gnas pa//rgyal ba kun kyang de 
3b7(rnying rgyud): bzhin mthun//phyag lag snol mar gnas pa yi//kun kyang sems dpa'i dkyil 'khor ro//phyag rgya thal mo rdo rje ni//kun kyang yum gyi rgyab tu bsnol//zhal gnyis mdzes par rab rgyas nas//zla gam pa yi ljags gnyis mdzes//dung gi phur 
4a1(rnying rgyud): @#//bus ma lur tshos la//gang bcing sdebs pa'i tshul du bzhugs//bsnyen pa nye bsnyen dbang rdzogs pa//rgyal ba kun gyi sku yi mchog/bzhad dang 'dzum pa'i gcog zungs bsgom//rnal 'byor dka' thub chen po'i don//gsang ba 
4a2(rnying rgyud): spyod la shin tu dgyes//gsang ba nyid du sems can 'dren//thams cad kun gyi rmongs pa sel//yum gyi las kyi khyad par ni//sa yi rim pa kun sbyong zhing //zas la bgo la dbang med pa//nyan thos 'gog pa la sogs pa//byang chub sems 
4a3(rnying rgyud): kyi char chen gyis//dam por bcings pa tshar gcod mdzad//dam tshig chen po sdom pa ste//rgyal ba kun gyi dkyil 'khor yin//phyag rgya chen po'i tshogs mchog ni//dag pa don gyi rigs mchog yin//myur bar rtag tu ma yengs na//gdon mi 
4a4(rnying rgyud): za bar 'grub pa ni//'dod khams lha yi nyin phyed kyis//gsang ba kun dang mnyam par 'gyur//de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsang ba mnyam pa zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//kun tu bzang po rnal 'byor mchog/gsang ba nyid kyi mkha' dbyings nas//
4a5(rnying rgyud): yi ge a ldan tsheg drag bcas//rnal 'byor bde chen thugs kyi sras//gzugs khams la sogs byin brlabs nas//phyag mtshan 'khor lor gsal ba dang //bde ba rnal 'byor de bzhin gshegs//bcom gser gyi kha dog la//nyi ma zer gyi 'od 
4a6(rnying rgyud): bzhin du//gsal ba 'tsher ba bcom ldan 'das//dbu ru yi ge gnyis pa las//rin chen myu gu bang pa 'bar//sangs rgyas lnga yi dbu rgyan gyis//legs par brgyan pa mtshan rnams rdzogs//rdzogs pa chen po'i bzhugs tshul dang //ting 'dzin 
4a7(rnying rgyud): mthun pas 'gro la dmigs//pad+ma ge sar dbus gnas pa'i//thig bzhi gsang ba bzhi dang ldan//mdun du lha mo chags pa mo//lo grangs nyi shu mdzes dang ldan//ma hA su ka'i gnas la dgyes//mdzes pa'i lag pa gar dang chas//
4b1(rnying rgyud): gnyis su med pa'i bzhugs tshul ni//gtso la brten pa'i tshul du bzhugs//rgyab tu lha mo dgyes chen mo//sangs rgyas brten par mdzad tshul gyis//thal mos 'dzin pa sku brtan pa//sku tshe bcu drug rtsa dmar sku//sangs rgyas gdung 'dzin chags 
4b2(rnying rgyud): pas sbyong //'dod chags can la mthun par mdzad//g.yas su zhe sdang gdul ba mo//rdo rje pad+mor sbyor la dgyes//ma dad pa rnams dbang med par//drag por sbyor mdzad zhe sdang sel//chags pa chen po bcom ldan 'das//gnyis su med par 
4b3(rnying rgyud): sbyor ba yis//gtong ba med par bstan pa'i tshul//sngo bsangs mdangs ldan khyad par che//khru tshad bcwa lnga chags pa can//dus mtha' med par sbyor la dgyes//steg dang sgeg pa'i cha lugs kyis//sems can kun gyi sdug bsngal 'khru//
4b4(rnying rgyud): rab tu skams pa 'gog pa rnams//rlang nas rgyal ba'i sras su 'gyur//a t+thA ma li'i phrin las kyis//thams cad byang chub chen por mdzad//mig gnyis 'dzums dang sbom po yis//dbang mor ldan par byas pa na//gnyis su med par 
4b5(rnying rgyud): sbyong 'dod yin//sna yi rtse mor mthe'u chung 'dzin//ma btang bzhugs la brtson byos pa'o//so rnams bsdams nas kha phye ba//dus la bab ces bya ba ste//ting 'dzin gsal bar rgyu ba na//dus la bab ces bya bayin//'di ltar brda ldan 
4b6(rnying rgyud): ting 'dzin la//dam can bud med mi 'dud pa//dngos grub mi 'gyur dam tshig nyams//tshor bas kun du brlag par 'gyur//'di ltar rtsen par byed pa na//g.yan pa bsgrung ba rnam bzhi yis//skyes bu na chung tshul bzhin du//rdo rje pad+ma'i 
4b7(rnying rgyud): mtshams nyid du//dbyings phyug lnga yi sbyor ldan pas//dal bus dal bus gzhug pa dang //dal bus dal bus rab tu bsgul//drag po'i yi ge spros pa na//byang chub sems kyis pad+ma dgang //sbom po dbang dam lnga rnams kyis//yum 
5a1(rnying rgyud): @#//gyis blangs te gtso la mchod//nye ba'i bsnyen pa la sogs pas//bsnyen pa che chung rdzogs par bsgom//byang chub sems kyi dkyil 'khor las//mchod pa brgyad kyis bstod par mchod//de ltar longs spyod rdzogs pa dang //gnyis 
5a2(rnying rgyud): su med par lung bzhin du//mnyam par 'khril ba'i tshul du bstan//dus gsum kun du gdung 'dzin pa//g.yon phyogs brten pa'i skal dang ldan//bod tshod lo ni bcu dgu pa'i//sdong po dul ba dag la dgyes//g.yas par bzung ba'i spyan gyis gzigs//
5a3(rnying rgyud): dril bu sgra snyan 'khrol la brtson//rdo rje rab tu sgrogs byed ma//sangs rgyas lnga yi dbu rgyan can//mngon du sbyor la rab brtson pa//brda dang bcas pa'i gar gyis ni//sangs rgyas kun la slong mdzad ma//byang chub sems kyi sprin chen gyis//mchod 
5a4(rnying rgyud): pa chen pos rab mchod mnyes//sprul pa bsam yas mtha' klas pa//dus gsum 'khor lo bskor bar bskul//ngo sor chags pa'i mdangs kyis ni//mnyes dang bcas pa'i gar gyis mchod//gru chad bzhi yi rim pa la//thos byed gsal 
5a5(rnying rgyud): byed gsung ba che//dri dang ldan pa la sogs pas//btsun mo dag dang thabs cig tu//gnyis su med pa'i tshul du bzhugs//bar khyams bzhi la gnas pa ni//thos byed dbang dang nyan byed dbang //dri mchog dbang du lta byed dbang //dus 
5a6(rnying rgyud): ni bzhi rnams lha mo bzhi//btsun mo'i tshogs dang ldan par gnas//zhe sdang 'joms byed la sogs pas//sgo rnams kun du gnas pa ni//snying rje'i yum dang bcas par gnas//nyi ma zla ba pad+ma mchog/rin chen dung dang bcas pa ste//
5a7(rnying rgyud): tshangs pa brgya byin gnyis gnyis kyis//bteg pa'i khri la rab tu bzhugs//rdzogs pa chen por kun kyang gnas//sems dpa' chen po'i bzhugs tshul du//kun kyang rab tu gnas par bstan//zhal ni kun du gsal le ba//
5b1(rnying rgyud): thugs ni kun du lhan ne ba//sku ni kun la khyab par gsal//e ma mi skye mi 'gag mchog/e ma mi sel mi len mchog/e ma tsham dam drag shul spyod//e ma gshegs pa'i stabs dang bcas//e ma mtshan ma sel bar ston//e ma bskyed 
5b2(rnying rgyud): rab rgya mtsho sgrogs//e ma byol song spro ba bskyed//e ma sprul pa bsam yas mchog/e ma mya ngan 'das par mdzad//e ma gsang ba des don mdzad//e ma gzungs dang mnyam par sbyor//e ma ci yang mi mdzad mchog/ngo mtshar 
5b3(rnying rgyud): sprin las 'di dag gis//sa yi rim pa 'byongs par 'gyur//zhes sangs rgyas pa nyid mngon par sangs rgyas pa la gleng ste mnyes par gyur to//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi gsang ba rnal 'byor bde ba chos kyi 'khor 
5b4(rnying rgyud): lo zhes bya ba'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das bde ba chen po kun du bzang mo rnal 'byor ma la 'jug par dgyes te rab tu 'dris pa chen po dgongs pa dang /kun du bzang mo rnal 'byorams/zhal 'dzum pa dang bcas pa/e 
5b5(rnying rgyud): ma sems dpa' thig le che//bag chags kun gyi gnyen por gsungs//'di las 'jug pa'i chos 'ga' med//zhes brjod do//de nas gsang ba chen mo dang gnyis su med par 'khril bar gyur te/g.yon pa bskyed pa rnam pa mang pos mnyes pa 
5b6(rnying rgyud): dang pad+ma sbas pa la sogs pa 'byung ba spel bar mdzad nas rab tu dgyes te glu 'di blangs so//e ma bde ba mchog las skyes pa phyag na rdo rje can//chos kun dngos po med par gsal ba la//'byung gnas chen pos ya mtshan 'byung bar mkhyen pa 
5b7(rnying rgyud): e ma ngo mtshar che//rgyu nyid stong pas 'bras bu btsal med pa'i//rgyu dang 'bras bu nyid kyang rgyal bas yong mi rnyed//stong pa nyid la dga' ba'i chags pa'i cho 'phrul ba//de phyir skye 'gag med ces rgyal ba rnams kyis 'di la brjod//chos kun dbyer 
6a1(rnying rgyud): @#//med ma rnyings pa nyid las//kun gyi dga' ba 'byung ba rgyal ba kun pas shin tu 'phags//zhes brjod pas gsang ba'i mkha' la kun du bzang po rnal 'byor 'jug par mdzad de shin tu rngam pa'i glu 'di blangs so//sgyu ma kun las 'phags pas 
6a2(rnying rgyud): dbyer med rnam par 'phrul byed pa//kun du bzang po rnal 'byorade ni bder gshegs yum chen kun gyi gsos//sdug bsngal kun sbyong bder gshegs yum la 'jug pa thugs dam yin zhes rgyal bas gsungs//oM hUM mi 'gog ji yang  
6a3(rnying rgyud): mdzad pa nyid ni mi mdzad mnyam pa yin zhes rgyal bas gsung //'di ni glu ru blangs pa yis//mya ngan 'das kun 'gro ba'i mgon//stong pa chos kyi dkyil 'khor la//dka' thub chen po thob par 'gyur//zhes gnyis su med par   
6a4(rnying rgyud): glengs so//de nas snying po mchog gi yi ge ni//dang po'i gnas su bsgom par bya//de snying yi ge bzhi pa ste//rdo rje chen po gsal ba mchog/yi ge a ldan thig le che//chos kun 'dus par rnam par bsgom//dang por me long lta bu ste//
6a5(rnying rgyud): mnyes pa'i kha dog shin tu 'bar//phyag lag 'khyud pa sprod pa yis//thams cad de dang 'dra bar gsal//oM hUM sur ta su ke dz+nyA na da oM AM hUM/bder gshegs rgyal mo mkha' dbyings su//gsal dag las rnams 'grub par bsgom//chu skyes    
6a6(rnying rgyud): pad+ma 'dab ma stong //bshad pa'i sgras ni rnam par brgyan//de steng chos kyi sku ru gsal//zla rgyas lta bu'i mkha' dbyings nas//thams cad bsdu ba gsum gyis bsdus//oM sur twa su khe d+harma d+hA tu swa b+hA ba oM AHhUM/gnyis su med pa'i yi ge mchog/
6a7(rnying rgyud): mi skye ba ni a dang ldan//byin brlabs rgyal ba kun gyi gnas//mchod pa brgyad dang gsal bar ldan//so sor gsal ba'i mkha' dbyings la//hUM gi zer gyis reg par mdzad//oM sur twa su khen pra dz+nyA b+ha k+Sha na swa b+hA ba oM AH hUM/
6b1(rnying rgyud): byang chub sems kyi char chen ni//yi ge gnyis pa rtsad mar sku//gnyis su med par ldan pa na//bdud kun rnam par btul ba dang //dbyings kyi bde la gsal ba ni//sgo dbye ba yi tshul gyis ni//pad+ma dbye ba sna tshogs bya//spro ba dang ni bsdu ba 
6b2(rnying rgyud): yis//bya ba nan tan ye shes bskyed//'di ltar rgyal ba kun mdzad pa//sprul pa bsam gyis mi khyab las//mchod pa mang po lha mor ldan//oM su ra twa su khe na du b+ha dz+nyA na badzra swa b+hA ba oM AHhU~M/rnal 'byor skal ldan chen po ni//skal ngan kun gyi 
6b3(rnying rgyud): gsos su gsungs//gnyis su med pa'i byang chub sems//maM sa bshad gci rdzas rnams ni//dogs pa med pa'i dngos grub ste//rgyal ba nyid la rgyal bas mchod//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi gsang ba'i snying po las bag chags gtan la 'bebs 
6b4(rnying rgyud): pa gnyis su med par gleng ba'i le'u ste gsum pa'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das gsang ba'i snying po rdo rje rnal 'byor ma dang gnyis su med pa'i phyag rgya'i mchog ni 'di lta ste/sgyu 'phrul dra ba'i phyag rgya las//mdzes par gsal ba yab 
6b5(rnying rgyud): yum bcu//sor mo phan tshun spel legs pa//rdo rje thal mo byang chub rgya//gsang ba rnal 'byor thugs kyi mchog/dam tshig sdom pa 'da' bar dka'//rnal 'byor pa la a lI gsal//kun du bzang la kar byed//lnga char zur du ldan 
6b6(rnying rgyud): pa ni//gcig tu gyur pas gar gyi gzhi//oM hU~M traM AHhU~M/mA A kA hU~M dA/thal mo btang snyoms tshul gyis ni//mdzes pa'i mtha' na gar gyis bya//hU~M dgar bcings pas rnal 'byor don//gar gyi phyag rgya dang nyid pas//gar du byas pas 
6b7(rnying rgyud): thal mo bsnol//mjug tu dbul ba'i tshul gyis bstod//rdo rje gar gyi phyag rgya las//g.yon pa rtse gsum g.yas pas gzung //de nyid gzhan du 'bigs par bya//rdo rje gar gyi phyag rgyal sa//dga' ba'i mdangs dang ldan pa yis//
7a1(rnying rgyud): @#//g.yas su 'dud pa'i tshul gyis ni//g.yas pa yi ni thugs kar bsgreng //g.yon pa gsung nyan 'khril ba'i tshul//rdo rje gar gyi phyag rgya las//mdzes pa'i mtha' nas gar byas shing //gzhan nas bsdus nas g.yas g.yon 'dzob//
7a2(rnying rgyud): rdo rje gar ni de dag las//lag pa g.yas pa 'degs pa dang //dbul ba'i tshul du rnam par brtag/rdo rje gar ni de dag las////spyan dang dbu ni g.yon du 'dud//cang te'u thogs pa'i tshul gyis ni//mchod pa byed par rnam par bsngags//rdo rje 
7a3(rnying rgyud): gar gyi phyag rgya las//lus ni g.yas g.yon bsgyur ba dang //gar las lag pa phral bskyed pa'o//rdo rje gar gyi phyag rgya pas//g.yon pas g.yas pa'i gru mo gzung //g.yas pa nyug pa'i tshul du bya'o//de nyid rdo rje gar dag las//dam tshig 
7a4(rnying rgyud): phyag rgya 'thor bar bstan//rdo rje gar gyi phyag rgya yis//dam tshig phyag rgya steng du bsgyur//de nyid g.yon pa'i nang dag las//g.yas pa'i dam tshig phyag rgyas gtor//de nyid g.yas pas drangs par ldan//de nyid gnyis pas gzung ba yin//de nyid 
7a5(rnying rgyud): gnyis ka thal mo bsdam//de nyid dril bu 'dzin par bsgom//oM sarba ta thA ga ta/mu ka ka ra su khe na oM/oM sarba ta thA ga ta sa ma rA ga s+t+waM su khe na hU~M/oM sarba ta thA ga te dz+wa la ya nA nA sa ma hA su khe na tAM/oM sarba ta thA gA ta d+harma b+ha shra su khe na hA hA hA/de nyid gtso bo'i 
7a6(rnying rgyud): phyag rgyar bcas//oM sarba ta thA gta sarba bud+d+ha lo tsA na At+ma na~M/su ra twa su khe na maN+Dal pU dza ya pU dza ya/oM oM aM/oM sarba ta thA ga tra ma hA d+hu dza dza/oM su ra twa su khe na b+han+d+ha b+han+d+ha/pU dza ya pU dza ya/hU~M hU~M hU~M A/oM su ra twa su kha ne/man sha mo ha/pU dza ya 
7a7(rnying rgyud): pU dza ya/hri hri hri/oM su ra twa su khen sarba ta thA ga ta d+harma dan+dA/sA d+ha kan da ho ho/oM sarba ta thA ga Sha shrI badzra lA s+ye/badzra ga d+he su khe na pU dza ya pU dza ya oM hU~M hU~M hU~M a/sarba ta thA ga ta shrI badzra ga d+he su khe na mU dza ya pU dza ya/traM traM A a/oM sarba ta thA ga ta shrI la dzra 
7b1(rnying rgyud): gan+d+he/badzra ga n+d+he su khe na/d+he pa d+hi pa/pU dza ya pU dza ya/hriM hriM hriM a/oM sarba ta thA ga ta shrI badzra ni ra te badzra ga n+d+he ma hA ti la ka su ke na pU dza ya pU dza ya/a a a/oM hU~M/oM sarba ta thA ga na shrI badzra d+hU be/badzra ga n+d+he su khi na pU dza ya pU dzA ya/oM ta da ta ta da hU~M/oM 
7b2(rnying rgyud): sarba ta thA ga ta shrIbadzra puSh+pe badzra ga n+d+he su khe na pU dza ya pU dzra ya/hU~M hU~M hU~M oM/oM sarba ta thA ga te shrI badzra a lo ka dz+nyA na maN+Da la ti la ka/bud+d+ha su khe na pU dza ya/oM hriM hriM hriM oM AHhU~M/oM sarba ta thAg ta shrI badzra gan+d+he/badzra ga n+d+hem hA maN+Da la ti la ka su khe na pU dza 
7b3(rnying rgyud): ya pU dza ya/a e a/oM AHhU~M/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad mnyes pa'i mchog las gar gyi le'u ste bzhi pa'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das dgyes pa chen mo dang bcas pa dam tshig bla na med pa'i rgyan bkod pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms 
7b4(rnying rgyud): par zhugs so//thams cad sdom pa la gnas par mkhyen no//dam tshig chen po 'di dag gsungs//so so'i theg pa bsam yas pa//so sor nyes pa med na bde//'jig rten pa la chad mdo btsan//'dul ba 'dzin la bsrungs na bde//gtsang sbrar 
7b5(rnying rgyud): spyod la gtsang sbrar spyad//mi dge bzlog na dge ba bcu//spyad pas lhag pa'i 'jig rten 'dzin//rang gi don gyi ting 'dzin gyis//byang chub sa la gnas pa'i rnams//nyams pa med de ma bcol na//nyams pa med de las kyang 'grub//rnal 'byor 
7b6(rnying rgyud): spyad pa spyod pas 'grub//tshig cis goms pas thog pa 'joms//gtso mchog bshes gnyen mi spong zhing //bkur sti rim gro mchog kyang bskyed//ting 'dzin goms par cha lugs bsgom//tshig gi bzlas pa bar mi gcad//dam tshig ma chad la gdung 
7b7(rnying rgyud): ba bskyed//sku yi dam tshig bsam mi khyab//gsung gi dam tshig bsngags pa yin//thugs kyi dam tshig lhag par gsungs//rigs lnga bsrungs pa'i dam tshig rnams//nyams na shin tu bskang bar dka'//spyad pa'i dam tshig rdzas 
8a1(rnying rgyud): @#//
8b1(rnying rgyud): bdun la brnyas//dkyil 'khor ba yi las byas pa//sems can dmyal ba chen po ste//tshe thung nad kyis btab pa dang //'jig rten 'di ru gnod pa mang //shi nas dmyal ba ngan song skye//de bas sdom pa 'di dag las//'da' bar mdzad pa gang yang med//
8b2(rnying rgyud): 'di las 'das par mi byed pas//tshe 'di nyid la rgyal ba'i sku//lhun gyis grub pa'i sa la gnas//zhes de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad nyid la nyid dam tshig chen po'i sdom pa 'dis shin tu bsnyengs par mdzad do//de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi gsang 
8b3(rnying rgyud): ba'i bde ba sdom pa thams cad kyi mchog gi le'u zhes bya ste lnga pa'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das kun du bzang po chen po rnal 'byor ma dang bcas pa dgongs pa gcig tu gnas par mdzad do//rnal 'byor gyi dbang phyug chen po spyad pa mchog dang 
8b4(rnying rgyud): ldan pa'i spyod pa ma lus pa nyid dang zab mo'i don yin par mkhyen to//mkhyen nas sgyu 'phrul chen mo'i thabs 'di gsungs so//e ma chos dbyings rnam dag spyod yul las//sangs rgyas sems can gnyis ston ya mtshan 'phrul chen yin//de yang ye shes 
8b5(rnying rgyud): tshogs las sna tshogs snang mod kyi//bud med kun gyi sgyu ma las ni rab tu sprul te 'char//bden gnyis so sor snang ba nyid kyang yum gyi mkha' zhes rgyal bas gsungs//ma nor lam chen sgom na kun du bzang mo bsgoms pas lam du zhugs pa yin//sangs 
8b6(rnying rgyud): rgyas kun gyi dgongs pa kun shes rab bud med las ni mchod cing bsnyen pas ye shes chen por 'grub bo//dkyil 'khor gdon pa'i las kyi dngos grub mchog gi la rdo rje glu'i don de nyid kyis gnyis su med pa'i dgongs pa la gsal bar gyur te/de nas de bzhin gshegs 
8b7(rnying rgyud): pa thams cad kyi bdag nyid dpal kun du bzang pos thabs dang shes rab kyi longs spyod 'di gsungs so//mngon spyod las kyi 'jug pa ni//phan tshun du ni dga' spyod pa//rnal 'byor sdom la gnas pa yin//de la mchod pa dam pa ni//sbyor ba rab tu sbyor ba ste//
9a1(rnying rgyud): @#//kun du khyab pa kun du sbyor//rjes su chags pa dam tshig mchog/de phyir kar sham mdog dang ldan//bcu drug bong tshod ring ba las//lugs su blugs pa lta bu ste//lus legs mdzes dang ldan pa dang //dpral ba dbyes che kos ko phra//nu ma chung 
9a2(rnying rgyud): zhing sor mo ring //yid tsam bzur la mig dkyus ring //dkar zhing rtsa dmar mdangs su chags//so dkar thags bzang 'tshams thung zhing //skad sbom gdangs dang ldan pa ni//rdo rje sems dpa'i gnas mchog ste//'di la rtse bar byed pa ni//rigs kyi 
9a3(rnying rgyud): rdo rje gdon mi za//tshe 'di nyid la 'grub par 'gyur//gnas rab dben par rab brtag ste//sha dkar mdangs ldan mdog dang ldan//lus 'bring zlum pa cha byad can//so dkar thags dam mig dkyus zlum//skad phra mdog dkar spro ring ba//dpral dbyes la 
9a4(rnying rgyud): sogs bzang gnas pa//rma dag gnas pa la sogs ni//rtag par chos la 'dud pa yin//tshangs pa dbyings kyi rigs can te//'di la brtson byas bsgrub pa yis//de bzhin rigs kyi ting 'dzin la//phan tshun du ni dga' spyad na//tshangs pa'i snga dro gcig tsam 
9a5(rnying rgyud): gyis//gdon mi za bar 'grub par 'gyur//rnal 'byor sbyor ba dga' ba ni//sbyor ba brtson pas 'grub par gsungs//bud med cha lugs legs pa ni//bod tshod mi ring lus phra zhing //rkang lag chung zhing skad gsal ba//mig legs bo zlum rtsa dmar che//
9a6(rnying rgyud): lhun du chags la mi shes med//lus la mtshan gyi rma 'dras brgyan//tshig gsal rmi lam nyung ba dang //song kar mchu legs dad par ldan//chos sems pa yi ting 'dzin gyis//gsang ba'i mkha' la nyi zla'i 'od//rdo rje pad+mar ldan byas 
9a7(rnying rgyud): te//phan tshun du ni dga' sbyad na//'dod khams lha yi skad cig gis//'grub par 'gyur ba gdon mi za//lus zlum brtun dang nga rgyal bcas//khong na tshig rnon sems la 'dzin//bya bar dran la mi gtong ldan//bslab pa blo rtul sha rus 
9b1(rnying rgyud): che//mdangs bkrag brtags na phyi phyir che//sbyin la dga' zhing chos la dad//rin chen rigs kyi ting 'dzin gyis//bag chags yi ges rtsad gcod cing //ting 'dzin blo la gsal ba na//bsgrub pa dus la bab pa ste//de tshe rab tu brtson pa yis//
9b2(rnying rgyud): lus kyi pad+ma kha bye ste//bde ba thugs kyi 'du 'gro yis//phan tshun du ni dga' spyad na//rigs kyi rin chen 'grub pa ni//tshe 'di nyid la thob par 'gyur//thugs rje mdzad spyod bye brag gis//'jig rten dag na dman pa'i rigs//rab tu bkug ste dga' spyod na//
9b3(rnying rgyud): lhang rje mchog dang thabs ldan pa//rnal 'byor spyod pa chen por gsungs//shan pa rigs ngan smad 'tshong ba//ngan pa'i rigs dang longs spyod rnams//'joms pa po yis ting 'dzin gyis//myur du spyad na 'grub par gsungs//yi dgas gnod sbyin byol song 
9b4(rnying rgyud): ba//khyi dang rus sbal spre'u la sogs//mi dang lha yi gnas mchog kun//so sor 'tsham par dga' spyad na//dregs pa can la zhi ba che//brtson la dga' ba brtson par mdzad//thabs kyi mdzad spyod kun gyi mchog/sems can 'di dag don gyi phyir//phyin ci 
9b5(rnying rgyud): log tu gang spyod rnams//'dul pa'i mdzad pa sna tshogs kyis//mi mdzad pa ni ci yang med//shin tu gtum pa'i cha lugs kyis//gtum po thams cad dbang du mdzad//shin tu zhi ba'i cha lugs kyis//dregs pa can rnams dbang du mdzad//shin tu/mdangs 
9b6(rnying rgyud): can cha lugs kyis//chags pa'i yid rnams dbang du mdzad/e ma shin tu ngo mtshar che//ci yang yin pas cir mi dgongs//zhes gsungs pas thams cad rab tu brtson pa chen po dang ldan par gyur to//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa bsam gyis 
9b7(rnying rgyud): ma khyab pa nam mkha' dang mnyam pa rdul phra mo'i rdul snyed rab tu 'dun pa'i thugs kyis mnyan pa brtson par mdzad do//rdo rje chos kyi mchog la sogs pa rigs bsam gyis mi khyab pa rab tu mnyan pa dang gus pa mchog dang ldan pas yongs su gzung ba'i le'u mdzad/
10a1(rnying rgyud): @#//de nas bcom ldan 'das rnal 'byor chen mo rab tu brtson pa mdzad/rab tu brtson pa'i yid kyis sprod pa dang 'khyud pa dang gyad kyi dgyes pa dang mgu ba bla na med pas phan tshun du dga' ba spyad do//de nas de bzhin nyid kyi dbyings nas sku dang gsung dang 
10a2(rnying rgyud): thugs rdo rje las 'di gsungs so//oM hUM ye shes rnal 'byor mchog ldan pas//skal bzang mchog gi dngos grub stsol//rab tu rmongs pa bsal phyir 'gog pa kun slong ba//rnal 'byor bde mchog gnyis su med pa 'di dag shin tu ngo mtshar che//rin chen 
10a3(rnying rgyud): yid bzhin gzungs ni kun gyi dbang ldan pa//tshangs ma'i mdangs ldan gzungs ma drangs te de brtson pas//grub par 'gyur ba 'di la dga' spyod a la la//shin tu srid pa 'di dag bzhin du spyad pas 'grub par gsungs pas sa la gnas pas kun pas 'phags/
10a4(rnying rgyud): rgyal ba kun gyi nang na kun du bzang po rgyal ba kun pas 'phags//de yang kun du bzang mo yin zhes sngon gyi rgyal ba kun gyis gsungs//mtshan ma kun bral rab tu dga' sbyor mi gtong bas//sems dpa' rdo rje nyid kyang de yin rgyal ba kun gyis gsungs //
10a5(rnying rgyud): tshangs pa mchog gi glu 'di dag/brtson par byed cing len pa ni//dka' thub rnal 'byor ting 'dzin te//tshangs pa mchog gi dro gcig gis//gdon mi za bar 'grub par gsungs//zhes brjod do//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad rab tu brtson pa chen pos 
10a6(rnying rgyud): gcig gi rang bzhin du gyur bar mdzad do//de bzhin gshegs ba thams cad kyi gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor zhes bya ba'i le'u ste drug pa'o////de nas dus gsum gyi ston pa thams cad bde bar gshegs pa'i rang bzhin te rab tu mchod pa chen pos mnyes 
10a7(rnying rgyud): shing ma rig pa brgyad khri bzhi stong 'dul bar mkhyen to//mkhyen nas ma rig pa grol bar mdzad pa dang zhe sdang rnam par grol bar dgyes te/de dag mnyam pa chen po'i rang bzhin gyis 'dul ba'i thabs su sgrol ba dang /dbang gi rigs kyi sbyor bas rab 
10b1(rnying rgyud): tu bsrungs shing bsrung ba mdzad do//bdag gi rnal 'byor kun du bzang //mtshan nyid kun brtags gnyis su med//rgyal ba kun dang mnyam sbyor na//mnyam pa'i sa la gnas pa yin//rang sems zla rgyas dbus gnas pa//rdo rje sems dpa'i ting 'dzin 
10b2(rnying rgyud): te//don rtogs gsal bar gyur pa yis//rab tu bsgral ba chen po ste//mchog gi sa la gnas rgyud yin//sku yi cho 'phrul rigs kun rdzogs//gsung gi ston pa bla na med//thugs kyi mdzad pa rtags dang ldan//ting 'dzin 'di ltar gsal ba yis//blo rmongs 
10b3(rnying rgyud): sdug bsngal sbyang ba'i phyir//gnyis su med par gsal ba yis//dzaHhU~M baM hos bsdu ba mdzad//mngon spyod las kyis sgrol ba mdzad//gnyis su med par 'grub par 'gyur//rtags dang shes pa phun sum tshogs//byams pa mdzad pa rnal 'byor thugs//gzungs 
10b4(rnying rgyud): kyi mchod pa mnyam ldan pas//rgyal ba mnyes par sbyor shes na//mnyes pa chen pos bdag nyid mchod//bden pa'i tshul du dbyer med pa'i//sems nyid rtogs pa rnam sbyang phyir//ting 'dzin shugs kyis kun bsgral te//mngon spyod las kyis grol bar mdzad//
10b5(rnying rgyud): gsang ba'i mkha' la kun tu sbyor//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi mdzad spyod bsam gyis mi khyab pa las mchod pa de kho na nyid de le'u bdun pa'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das 'khor rab tu skyong ba la dgyes te/slob ma mchog dang 'bring dang tha mas yon gyi dbul 
10b6(rnying rgyud): ba mang po byas te dkyil 'khor du gzung ba mdzad do//bgegs char gcad pa'i phyir rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis gdong par bstan rin po che sa la gsor bkang ste snying po 'di zlos su gzhug go//oM sarba ta thA ga ta pU dzi d+hi d+ha tiSh+Tha hU~M//oM ma hA maN+Da la de ba kar 
10b7(rnying rgyud): pU dza ya pU dza ya hU~M a/zhes brjod de me tog 'thor du gzhug go//gang la bab pa'i bshes gnyen bstan//sngags dang phyag rgya dam tshig bstan//de nas dam tshig chu klung cing //tshig 'di rab tu go bar bsgo//sems can gyi ni gzugs chen po//las 
11a1(rnying rgyud): @#//la sogs te dbang bskur ba//rgyal ba'i sras su dbang bskur yin//de ring phan chad bu khyod kyis//dam tshig gnyen po 'di dag ni//srog gi phyir yang mi gtong zhing //slob dpon ma btang grogs la mchod//bka' la gus par mnyan par byos//
11a2(rnying rgyud): gal te bka' las 'das gyur na//gzhom dang gzhig dang dam rtsal dang //spro dang spar dang dmyal bar skye//sems la chu 'di gnas pa ni//phrin las rnam bzhi 'tsham par bya//zhes brjod nas/rdo rje slob dpon dkyil 'khor dang //kun du bzang dang mched 
11a3(rnying rgyud): la sogs//zhabs la gtugs dang gus par bya//rgyud kyi mnyan pa thams cad bya//bsgrub pa'i gzugs la brten pas 'grub//de nas rigs kyi bu mchog la//gsang ba'i ming gi dbang yang bskur//gus par ldan pa'i bu mchog des//rgyal 
11a4(rnying rgyud): po dbang yod rgyal po srid//rang lus gnas dang 'khor yul dang //bu dang chung ma tsha rgyud dang //rin po che dang shel dang dngul//sna bdun rin chen nor gyi dbyig/rta dang glang po shing rta dang //ma he ne'u le ci yod rnams//gter dang dbyig dang yid 'thad 
11a5(rnying rgyud): pa//sems la the tshom med pa yis//bkur sti rim gro mchog bskyed la//phul na rgyal ba mnyes par 'gyur//de nas dbang mnod theg pa'i tshad//rim pa bzhin du mnod par bya//mnyan pa grub pa la sogs dang //las dang ging dang rgyal 'the ba dang //
11a6(rnying rgyud): spyan dang rdo rje tshad 'dzin pa//bye brag mngag pa la sogs rnams//gsang ba'i dbang rnams gang nod pa//nyi shu 'am bcu gnyis lon pa la//gos dang dbu rgyan bza' btung dang //phyag rgya dril bu la sogs pa//dkyil 'khor 'di ltar gsal bsgoms te//rgyal 
11a7(rnying rgyud): ba kun gyis byin gyis brlabs//mngon du stan bting dga' spyod cing //steng nas rgyal pos dbang bskur na//gdon mi za bar dngos grub thob//tshig gi legs pa bstod par bya//de nas rigs kyi bu mchog des//zhabs la gtugs te gtang rag gtong //rdo rje 
11b1(rnying rgyud): spun dang gzungs ma yis//rgyal ba'i rgyal ba yin zhes brjod//dus gsum kun gyi sras chen po//dus gsum kun gyi spyad pa spyod//khams gsum kun gyi sgrol ba po//sgrol ba mchog gi spyad pa spyod//shin tu rnal 'byor skal ldan pa//dam tshig sdom la 
11b2(rnying rgyud): gnas pa dang //mtshan ma med pa'i chu 'thung zhing //ji ltar bsgo ba'i las byed pa//kun la khyad par rab grags yin//phyir bas 'dren pa'i gdung 'dzin dang //sgyu 'phrul dra ba'i sa la gnas//de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi gsang ba de kho na 
11b3(rnying rgyud): nyid las mchod yon dam pa dang snying rje'i le'u ste brgyad pa'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das gsang ba rdo rje bkod pa'i rgyan ces bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs te gnas par mdzad do//mdzad nas sprel zhi dang bya byed yan man mang po mi 'gog 
11b4(rnying rgyud): pa la snyoms par zhugs so//de nas yang las la ltos pa'ang thad kyi sdug bsngal rnams mi g.yo ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis 'jog par mdzad do//de nas yang mu stegs rtag par lta ba ther zug pa la rtag pa ther zug gi cha lugs kyis 'dul bar mdzad do//chad 
11b5(rnying rgyud): pa phyis mi 'dod ces bya ba la cha lugs des mnyam pa la 'dzud par mdzad do//mdzad na rab tu 'gog pa'i dka' thub kyis myur ba bde ba la gnas par mi ster ba rnams la dka' thub 'gyur ba chen po'i tshul gyis 'dul dbang chen po mdzad do//drag shul gyi btsan 
11b6(rnying rgyud): thabs la dga' ba de thags zangs ris kyi cha lugs kyis 'dul bar 'dzad do//gzhan la snying rjer ma gyur pa'i phyogs gcig pa'i sa rgyal rnams la yid bzhin gyi ting nge 'dzin gyis 'dul bar mdzad do/gling bzhi'i dge sdig cha mi shes pa la 'khor los sgyur ba 
11b7(rnying rgyud): chen por mdzad nas 'dul bar mdzad do//sgre bas pa chen po dang gtub pa chen po dang /btso ba dang rab tu so tham tha ma che chung rnams gnyen po dang zangs kha dang /gshed po chen po'i tshul gyis 'dul bar mdzad do//de dag kyang chos mnyam pa chen po byang 
12a1(rnying rgyud): @#//chub kyi tshul rdo rje theg pa kun gyi mchog rang bzhin gyi tshul gyis gnas pa thabs dang shes rab dbyer med de rol ba bsam gyis mi khyab pa la gnas par mdzad do//thabs kyi mdzad pa khro bo rnam par 'phrul pa zhes bya ba'i phyag rgya'o//de nas bcom ldan 
12a2(rnying rgyud): 'das kun du bzang po chen po bdud 'dul ba zhes bya ba'i rnam par 'phrul ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs par mdzad do//de nas bdud zhe sdang chen po zhes bya ba de bskal pa stong gi dus na sdug bsngal bsam gyis mi khyab pa blangs pas byang chub tu sems ma bskyed pa/
12a3(rnying rgyud): rab tu gdug pa/skyi bung ba/bong che zhing tshor sang che ba/mtshon cha dang khrag gi gdug pas sems can la gnod pa/mtshon gyi mche ba dang bcas pa dpag tshad nas dri tshor te rab tu gsod gcod za 'thung byed cing me dpung gi dkyil na gdug pa'i mda' 'phen cing g.yo ba 
12a4(rnying rgyud): chen pos bsngo ba drag po dang tsham ngam du gnas par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das cang mi gsung bas kun du bzang mo la 'dzum pa mdzad do//yab yum gyi byang chub kyi sems kyi sprin las/oM d+hu d+hu d+hu d+hu ra d+hu ra d+hu ra ho ho/hU~M hU~M hU~M/hriM hriM hriM/ho ho 
12a5(rnying rgyud): zhes brjod pas tshangs pa'i chung ma dang /dbang po'i chung ma dang /khyab 'jug gi chung ma dang /tsakra'i chung ma dang /de ba'i chung ma dang /ru tra'i chung ma dang /ga ru Ta'i chung ma dang /yul 'khor srung gi chung ma dang /rnam thos kyi chung ma dang /'phags skyes

57b3(rnying rgyud): cig car don nyid rdzogs//ma bsgrubs rgyal ba kun gyi gtso//ma sbyangs spyon dang yon tan med//tshig dang yi ges mi mtshon te//sangs rgyas nyid kyis rnyed par dka'//kun bzang rdo rje sems dpa' yis//rnyed nas rdzogs pa'i dkyil 'khor spyod//
57b4(rnying rgyud): spyod pas 'gro ba sgrol ba ste//de la nyes pa'i skyon mi mnga'//zhes gsungs pas//phyogs bcu dus bzhi'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dbyer med par dgyes nas stug pos brgyan pa'i rgyan nyid du bzhugs so//sgyu 'phrul dra ba bla ma chen po las man ngag snying po 
57b5(rnying rgyud): byang chub sems bde ba chen po'i le'u ste bcu gcig pa'o////de nas yang ye shes rgyal po de nyid du bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi dgongs pa yang dag pa'i phrin las dang chen po 'di ma byon pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa rnams gyi ched du sku gsung 
57b6(rnying rgyud): thugs yon tan 'phrin las rdo rje las phyung ngo//e yi tshogs kyi dkyil 'khor mchog/'bar ba gtum chen rngam pa la//khros pa'i yid kyis mchod sbyin bya//sangs rgyas bsod nams sku yang 'jig/bdud rtsi lnga 'am zas lnga la//mtshan ma ming du bcas 
57b7(rnying rgyud): par bya//bkug nas rdo rje phur pas gdab//rdul du byas nas tshogs la 'bul//e yi tshogs kyi dkyila'akhor du//lu gu rgyud du sbrel bsdams nas//bcings gyur bskyod nas phyogs bcur g.yos//myos 'gyur gas te tshig 
58a1(rnying rgyud): @#//par 'gyur//paM gyi tshogs kyi dkyil 'khor mchog/'bar ba'i gzi brjid lhun chen la//chags pa'i yid kyis mchod sbyin bya//rdo rje gsum yang dbang du 'gyur//bdud rtsi lnga'am zas lnga la//mtshan ma ming du bcas par bya//rdo rje chags 
58a2(rnying rgyud): pa'i phur pas gdab//chags pa'i tshogs la thim par dbul//paM gyi tshogs kyi dkyila'akhor du//lu gu rgyud du sbrel bsdams nas//ci 'dod de bzhin byed par 'gyur//ma yi tshogs kyi dkyil 'khor mchod//'bar ba'i 'du 'phro'i gzi chen la//
58a3(rnying rgyud): dga' ba'i yid kyis mchod sbyin bya//yon tan nam mkha'i mtha' dang mnyam//bdud rtsi lnga'am zas lda la//yon tan yid bzhin gter du brtag/nga rgyal dga' 'phro'i phur pas gdab//rdo rje gzi brjid 'phel bar brtag/ma yi tshogs kyi dkyil
58a4(rnying rgyud): 'khor du//lu gu rgyud du sbel bsdams nas//gzi brjid 'bar ba'i 'phrul chen spro//yid bzhin nam mkha'i mtha' dang ma nyam//ya yi tshogs kyi dkyil 'khor 'chog/gsal ba'i gzi brjid 'tsher ba la//dang ba'i yid kyis mchod sbyin ba//gtum 
58a5(rnying rgyud): chen rngam po'ang lha ner 'gyur//bdud rtsi lnga'am zas lnga la//gtum chen 'khrug pa'i ngo bor brtag/lhan ner gsal ba'i phur pas gdab//'bar ba lha ne'i tshogs la 'bul//ya yi tshogs kyi dkyila'akhor du//lu gu rgyud du sbrel bsdams nas//'bar ba lha 
58a6(rnying rgyud): ne'i byin gyis khyab//thams cad g.yo ba med par 'gyur//a/thams cad rdzogs pa chen po ste//las su ma gyur gcig kyang med//las kyi khyad par rdzogs pa yi//srid pa gsum du bsdus pa rnams//byin gyis ma brlabs gcig kyang med//rdzogs pa 
58a7(rnying rgyud): chen po'i phrin las kyis//sbyangs pas bsam rdzogs lhun gyis grub//ho/rlas dang byi mo bran mo'i tshogs//lhag ma kha chu 'dod pa sbyin//rang gi dam tshig rab bsgrubs te//gang 'dod las de byed par bsgo//khrag 'thung chen po la sogs te//bro gar glu tshig 
58b1(rnying rgyud): chen mos ni//ji ltar 'dod pa thams cad kun//thams cad ma lus byed pa yin//mdzod ldan chen mo la sogs te//bro gar glu tshig chen mos ni//'du bar 'dod na 'du bar byed//'phel bar 'dod na 'phel bar byed//spos mo chen mo la sogs te//bra gar 
58b2(rnying rgyud): glu tshig chen mos ni//rmongs par 'dod na rmongs par byed//myos par 'dod na myos par byed//seng gdong chen mo la sogs te//pro gar glu tshig chen mos na//thams cad kun la za bar byed//rdul cha tsam yang med par byed//bzhad gdong chen mo la sogs ste//
58b3(rnying rgyud): phro gar glu tshig chen mos pa ni//thams cad kun la 'byin par byed//kun la rab tu nyams par byed//a/spor byed chen mo la sogs te//nyul byed chen mo brgyad rnams la//bro gar glu tshig che na mos ni//thams cad kun las nyul bar byed//ma lus 
58b4(rnying rgyud): gnas nas spor bar byed//slas dang phyi mo bran mo'i tshogs//bro gar glu tshig chenamos ni//sbad cing gtang por bya ba ste//thams cad ma lus byed pa yin//ljon shing dang ni lcug phran tshal//sdong gcig dang ni me reg tu//dang ba dga' ba chags pa dang //thod 
58b5(rnying rgyud): pas ka li rab tu bya//zhes gsungs pas phrin las rnam pa bzhi sti ra'i rnga dang bcas par phyogs bcu 'jig rten gyi khams 'dul bar gyur te/de nas bcom ldan 'das ye shes rgyal po 'jigs byed chen po de nyid dam tshig sti ra'i rnga bla ma rdung zhing sti ra rngam pa'i 
58b6(rnying rgyud): mdangs 'di phyung ngo //e yi tshogs chen 'byam pa ru//sti ra'i rnga chen sgra bsgrags pas//hU~M ha he phaT sgra 'byin cing //yig 'bru brgyad kyi byin brlabs kyis//ru lu hU~M b+h+yo'i gsung sgrogs pa//khro bo rdam pa'i sgra skad kyis//srid pa gsum chang 
58b7(rnying rgyud): bshig nas kyang //stong gsum bsgral ba'i sgra 'byin pa'o//sti ra rnga chen 'di brdungs pas//gnod sbyin srin po kun bsgral te//mu stegs kun gyi snying yang 'byin//sti ra 'di chen 'di bsgrags pas//stong gsum khro bo'i tshogs kyis bkang //kun kyang 
59a1(rnying rgyud): @#//rnga brdung di ri ri//rnga sgra chen pos stong gsum bshig/rnga sgra chen pos bdud dpung bsgral//rnga sgra chen pos srid gsum sbyangs//rnga sgra chen pos dgra bgegs 'dul//rnga sgra chen pos ngan song sbyangs//rnga sgra 'brug gi sgra bsgrags pas//na rag 
59a2(rnying rgyud): dmyal khams stongs par byed//rnga sgra tug chem ri rab rnyil//sti ra rnga chen sgra phyung bas//phyin ci log rnams snying yang 'byin//rnga chen 'brug gi sgra sgrogs shing //rnga thabs glog stong 'gyur ba bzhin//de bzhin gshegs pa'i thugs dam bskang //ma mo 
59a3(rnying rgyud): mkha' 'gro'i dam tshig bskang //rnal 'byor rnam lnga'i dam tshig bskul//sti ra rnga chen 'di brdungs pas//nyan thos mdo sde gnas nas spar//rang rgyal lta ba dbyings su sgrol//sti ra'i rnga chen 'di bsgrags pas//lha dang mi yi theg pa yang //bla med 
59a4(rnying rgyud): theg pa'i dbyings su btsud//sti ra rnga sgra 'di bsgrags pas//stong khams sti ra'i rnga sgras bkang //kun kyang rnga brdung di ri ri//rnga thabs glog stong lce 'byin cing //rnga skad 'brug stong sgra sgrogs pas//byin brlabs lcags kyi ser chen 'bebs//stong gsum 
59a5(rnying rgyud): 'khrug cing srid gsum 'thor//rnga thabs g.yab mor bor ba dang //kha dang ka yi ru mtshon gyis//thams cad gnam lcags chen pos btab//ka la chen po la sogs te//rdo rje chags 'og ldegs pa'i rnams//dus gcig tu ni tsit+ta 'byin//sti 
59a6(rnying rgyud): ra chen po'i rnga sgra yis//srid pa khrag gi rgya mtshor bskyil//dus gcig tu ni dbang chen rdzogs//rnga sgra rin chen khang pa brtsegs//bsam rdzogs che la dbang thang thob//rnga sgras g.yo ba med par byed//chos la 'dzud pa'i sgra yang 'byin//rnga sgras ci 'dod 
59a7(rnying rgyud): dgongs pa rdzogs//byang chub sems lhar de ma thag/ces gsungs pas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad de bzhin gshegs pa nyid kyi sbyor bar gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das dgyes pa chen po ye shes rgyal po de nyid kyi dkyil 'khor bla ma chen po man 
59b1(rnying rgyud): dag gi snying po dang bcas pa de la shin tu rngam pa'i mdangs kyis dgyes pa chen po'i glu 'di blangs so//hUM gtum chen dus mtha' mi ltar 'bar//'od zer nyi ma 'bum gyi gzi//khro gnyer glog stong 'gyur ba bzhin//mche ba zang yag za byed che//ho/
59b2(rnying rgyud): hUM rngam pa'i nga ro 'brug stong ldir//tug chem ri rab 'bum rnyil skad//a la la li gad rgyangs che//spyugs pa'i 'thor rlung g.yeng ba che//ho/hUM khro bo'i shes rab 'od po che//ye shes dkyil 'khor kun du gsal//'bar ba'i ye shes kun du 'dzems//
59b3(rnying rgyud): sna tshogs ye shes thig le che//ho/hUM khro ba'i rgyal po sprin chen po//khros pa'i dkyil 'khor char chen 'bebs//dkyil 'khor yid bzhin 'byung ba'i gter//sna tshogs khros pa'i thig le che//ho/hAMu bdud rnams kun gyi bdud chen po//
59b4(rnying rgyud): bdud kyi bdud ste bdud rnams 'joms//'jigs pa'i tshogs kyang 'jigs byed pa//'jigs byed chen po bdal ba che//hUM rdo rje brag chen sra ba po//rdo rje chu bo spud chen po//rdo rje me ste kun 'bar che//rdo rje rlung ste 'thor rlung che//ho/hUM stong gsum khro 
59b5(rnying rgyud): bo'i dkyil 'khor tshogs//'jigs byed mtshon du med pa'i sku//sna tshogs gsung dang sna tshogs thugs//'jigs byed rngam pa'i ye shes gter//ho zhes blangs pas stong gsum gyi de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'jigs byed chen po rnams dbyer med pas 
59b6(rnying rgyud): mnyes par gyur to//sgyu 'phrul dra ba bla ma chen po las lhun gyis grub pa'i phrin las dang yongs su mnyes pa'i le'u ste bcu gnyis pa'o////rdo rje sems dpas gsungs pa'i don//gsang ba kun gyi bla ma ste//sgyu 'phrul dra ba'i rgyal po yin//des don chos 
59b7(rnying rgyud): kyi snying po las//'dul ba mdo sde chos mngon dang //dam tshig sgrub dang grub pa dang //sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi rgyud//sgyu 'phrul dra ba las 'phros te//sde snod kun dang rgyud kun yang //'byung nas gtan la nges par 'bebs//'gro drug sems 
60a1(rnying rgyud): @#//can gsum po yang //stan chen po'i dbyings su bstan//ling ga padamar bcas pa yang //gan chen po'i dbyings su sbyor//snang srid kha dog yang po yang //gan chen po'i lam du bstan//rang bzhin gsang ba'i snying po 'di//rtogs nas 
60a2(rnying rgyud): smra bar gang byed pa//de nyid nga yin dbang yang rdzogs//rtag tu byang chub sems la gnas//tshe 'di nyid kyis mngon sangs rgyas//nor bu rin chen 'bar ba ltar//bsam pa thams cad yid bzhin 'grub//cig gsungs pas phyogs bcu dus gsum gyi de bzhin 
60a3(rnying rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad dbyer med par dgyes te/mnyam pa nyid kyi dbyings na lhun stug po bkod pa'i rgyan nyid du bzhugs so//gsang ba'i snying po de kho na nyid des pa'i sgyu 'phrul bla ma chen po las gdeng chen po'i le'u ste bcu gsum pa'o////
60a4(rnying rgyud): rtogs pa'i rgyal po sgyu 'phrul dra ba'i le'u stong phrag brgya pa las lung gi spyi sangs rgyas thams cad kyi gsang ba gsang ba'i snying po de kho nanyid des pa'i bla ma chen po'i le'u las thams cad ma lus par 'phros pa de dag gi 'bras bu'i mchog rdzogs so////
60a5(rnying rgyud): @#//rgya gar skad du/Ar+ya many+dzu shrI ka rma tsa tu sh+tsa kra gu h+ya tan+t+ra/bod skad du/'phags pa 'jam dpal las bzhi 'khor lo gsang ba'i rgyud/'phags pa 'jam dpal ye shes sems dpa' la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos 
60a6(rnying rgyud): pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das dgyes pa chen por gyur te/yum chen mo dang gnyis su med pa'i mkha' la bzhugs pa la 'di lta ste/'khor khro bo dang /khro mo dang /ma mo dang /mngag gzhug dang /'bangs la sogs pa rnams kyis/
60a7(rnying rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa chen po la 'dud pa'i tshul du bzhugs so//de nas bcom ldan 'das btsun mo dang gnyis su med bar gyur/skye 'gag dang bral ba'i don 'di ston to//ye nas ma skyes gshin rje chen po'i tshogs//rnam dag dbyings 
60b1(rnying rgyud): kyi rang nyid rnam par dag pa la//gnyis med 'dus pa'i sras mchog gshin rje gshed//thabs dang shes rab rnam par dag pa yi//kun gyi ye shes thams cad 'dus pa ste//thugs rje chen pos 'gro ba'i don mdzad pa//sems can 
60b2(rnying rgyud): ma lus mya ngan 'das pa'i lam//ye shes thams cad de yi ngang la rdzogs//'khor ba'i nyes skyon thams cad tshar gcad phyir//'dod chags 'dus pa'i dpal ldan gar gyi bdag/bye ba phrag brgya dpag tu med par yang //'dul ba'i dbang 
60b3(rnying rgyud): gis de lta bu yi gzugs su bstan//zhes gsungs pas 'khor gyi khro bo dang khro mo la sogs pa thams cad mgu bar gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das 'jam dpal gshin rje gshed chen po la 'di skad ces gsol ba btab bo//sems can 'khor bas 
60b4(rnying rgyud): gdungs pa la//mya ngan 'das pa'i lam mchog bstan//bstan pa dam pa 'di gsungs pas//thams cad sgrol ba ngo mtshar che//zhes gsol to//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad yum dang gcig tu 'dus te/'di skad ces gsungs 
60b5(rnying rgyud): so//sprul pa'i sku dag tshad med dpag tu med//thun mong ma yin 'jam dpal gshin rje gsher//sku gsung thugs kyi phrin las rmad du byung //mi dge bcu dang mtshams med tshar gcad pa//zug rngu mi mnga' chos nyid rmad po che//
60b6(rnying rgyud): chos kyi sku dang longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku//sprul pa'i sku dang gsum ni don dam kun du sbyor//phyogs bcu bde bar gshegs pa thugs kyi dpal//yang dag ye shes dpal yin 'jam dpal yin//zhes bstod de gnyis su med par gyur to//de nas de 
60b7(rnying rgyud): bzhin gshegs pa dam pa de la 'khor gyis de bzhin du gsol ba la dgyes te/'di skad ces gsung so//zhi ba gnyis med chos kyi ngang nyid las//snying po gnyis med byang chub sems dpa'i don//thams cad 'dus pa'i bla med dam pa ste//de bzhin 
61a1(rnying rgyud): @#//gshegs pa'i snying po 'di bshad do//oM sarba ta thA ga ta e h+ye hi ta thA ga ta dra ba ti/bo d+hi satwa d+harma d+hA tu/e h+ye hi dzaHhU~M h+ye hi h+ye hi hU~M hU~M hU~M/zhes brjod nas brdas mtshon par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das steng gi phyogs su sems can thams cad 
61a2(rnying rgyud): sgrol ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs so//snyoms par zhugs pas phyogs la phyogs su bltas te/rang bzhin gyis sgrol ba'i nus pa can du gyur te/thams cad kyang sgrol bar gyur to//de nas yum gyi tshogs thams cad kyis dgyes pa chen po'i brda 
61a3(rnying rgyud): 'dis bcom ldan 'das la bskul lo//e ma'o phyogs bcu dus gsum rdzogs pa yi//ye shes 'dus pa'i phrin las mchog//bla med theg pa nges pa la//sbyor ba mchog gis bshad du gsol//zhes bskul bas/phyogs bcu'i de bzhin gshegs pa 
61a4(rnying rgyud): thams cad dgyes par gyur to//de nas phyogs bcu'i de bzhin gsheg pa rnams dbang phyug gi 'khor mya ngan las 'das pa thams cad dang bcas nas sku'i rgyan gyis brgyan te/so so'i btsun mo'i dgyes pas sku'i nyams su bstar bar gyur to//
61a5(rnying rgyud): de ltar gyur bas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad ba spu langs te 'di skad ces ched du brjod do//sku gsung thugs rnams 'gro ba so sor 'dul//ngang gi zhi ba'i dbyings las ma g.yos kyang //phrin las rnam bzhis 'gro ba so sor 'dul/de phyir 
61a6(rnying rgyud): mchog gi rgyal po 'di bshad do//zhes gsungs pas/'khor rnams rab tu dga' mgur rangs par gyur te 'di skad ces gsol to//sku gsung thugs kyis 'gro ba rang rang 'dul//dbyings las 'dren pa rdo rje gsor byas te//'di ltar 'dul bar mdzad 
61a7(rnying rgyud): pa ngo mtshar che//zhes brjod do//sku la zhugs nas mi snang bar gyur to//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad khro bos 'dul bar mkhyen nas 'di skad ces gsungs so//khra dang smyung ki srin mo mu stegs rnams//bdud dang yog 'dren thams cad 
61b1(rnying rgyud): tshar gcad phyir//chos nyid sgyu ma lta bu sprul nas phrin las mdzad//ces gsungs pas/'khor rnams ngo mtshar du gyur te/'di skad du/e ma'o che ba'i yon tan kun la khyab pa la//ngang gis khro bor bstan pa 'di lta bu//ngo mtshar rmad byung sprul pa 
61b2(rnying rgyud): 'di lta bu//rab tu mi srun 'dul ba ngo mtshar che//zhes gsol to//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis ma bskul bar 'di skad ces gsungs so//khyad par rim par bstan pa'i khro gnyer ni//ye shes dbyings kyis kun la khyab par ston//khro bo bzhi dang 
61b3(rnying rgyud): brgyad dang bcu la sogs//ston pa mchod phyir khro bo mang po bstan//dang khro kun khyab shugs khro rang rang 'dul ba yi//bye brag so sor rab tu shes bya la//mtshan ma drag po zhi ba dbang dang rgyas//go rims bzlog ste rab tu bshad par bya//lte bar bcas pa'i 
61b4(rnying rgyud): rtsibs can la//'khor ba gcod byed rtse mo can//gru gsum e yi pho brang a//zlag ma rnam gsum dbang gi tshul//mu khyud dad can rgyas pa phyi//rnam bzhir ldan pa'i 'khor lo la//rab tu mdzes pa'i thig gdab bo//dbus su ma he'i zhing gdan la//rtse dbus 
61b5(rnying rgyud): zhing rkyang re re'o//rus pa tshogs kyi dpa' rlabs khrag tu spungs//rak+ta khrag gi dpa' klong mtsho lta bu//gnam lcags dur mo rab tu ta la la//dur khrod rab tu brgyan pa de bsham mo//dbus su khro rgyal gshin rje gshed//sku ni nag po dug 'dra la//dbu gsum dkar 
61b6(rnying rgyud): dmar mthing ba ste//ral pa kham nag srid rtser 'bar//rtse la me 'bar rked nas zhag kyang 'dzag/rtsa ba chu ste sdang mig rab tu bgrad//phyag drug phyag mtshan bsnams pa'i thor tshugs ni//sdigs mdzub rgyu zhags lcags kyug yas//dung chen mdung thung gsal shing

88b1(rnying rgyud): zlog/rig pa'i skyes bu rnam par grags//zhes pas dgyes par gyur to//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dgongs pa bla na med pa'i gsang ba dpal rta mchog rol pa'i rgyud las/de kho na nyid bstan pa'i le'u ste bzhi pa'o////de nas rig 
88b2(rnying rgyud): pa'i skyes bu mchog/gsang ba'i bdag pos yang zhus pa//bcom ldan snying po byang chub sems//dgongs pa'i don ni ci lta bu//las dang nyon mongs dag byed pa'i//ye shes sgron ma ci ltar lags//rgyal bas dgongs pa'i byang chub sems//
88b3(rnying rgyud): rdo rje lta bur mnyam bzhag nas//mi rtog ye shes gsal mdzad de//sna tshogs rnams kyang byang chub sems//bde ba che las gzhan med do//lus ngag yi dag sum rnam pa'i tshogs//chos rnams kun gyi rgyu yin te//rnam gsum rdo rje lta bu nyid//
88b4(rnying rgyud): sku gsum gsal ba rnam par snang //dngos po gsal ba'i chos med de//gcig las rnam 'phrul sna tshogs nyid//phyag rgya gar thabs dbang bsgyur bas//'ching ba'i tshogs las rnam par grol//dngos po thams cad bral ba yi//phung khams 
88b5(rnying rgyud): skye mched rnam par dben//gzung 'dzin rtog pa'i yul bral bas//dpag med pa yi ngo bo nyid//rnam gsum mchog gi don las byung //'od gsal chen po'i rang bzhin no//ngo mtshar rmad du byung ba'i don//rgyal ba kun gyi gsang ba'i thugs//
88b6(rnying rgyud): skye dang skye med sna tshogs dngos//phyogs 'dzin mtha' yis bcing bar byed//de dag chos las rnam grol ba'o//bden pa chen po'i ting 'dzin rgya//ngo mtshar ya mtshan rmad kyi chos//me long gsal ba'i rang bzhin can//rgyal ba rnams kyi gsang ba'i 
88b7(rnying rgyud): mdzod//'gag pa med pa'i chos rnams la//byang chub sems nyid skye bar 'gyur//mnyam nyid de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku//ya mtshan rmad du byung ba'i chos//sna tshogs snang byed gnas grol ba//rang bzhin zhi ba'i byang chub sems//gnyis kyi mtha' las grol ba'o//gnas 
89a1(rnying rgyud): @#//dang gnas med byang chub sems//ya mtshan rmad kyi don byung ba'o//rgya cher so sor gsal ba'i ngang //kun rtogs byang chub sems chen po//dmigs med gsang ba las byung ba'i//dmigs dang mi dmigs sna tshogs ni//bde ba'i ye shes gsal 
89a2(rnying rgyud): ba'o//ma 'gags pa yi chos nyid do//bya ba grub pa'i tshul can du//spros med spros pa sna tshogs snang //spro bsdu 'dun dang spros bral ba'i//mdzad pa'i byang sems brtan pa las//ngo mtshar cho 'phrul bla na med//ye shes rgya mtsho rmad du byung //rang gi 
89a3(rnying rgyud): de nyid gsal bar 'gyur//rgyal bas bshad pa'i dgos don la//'khor rnams ya mtshan ngoms par 'gyur//mchod dang zhal dag sgra 'byin cing //rang gi de nyid gsal 'gyur nas//byang sems de nyid bstod par bya//e ma'o stong nyid byang chub sems//
89a4(rnying rgyud): e ma'o dag pa dri ma med//e ma mi sgrib gsal ba che//e ma'o mnyam nyid dbyer med chos//e ma'o so so rang rig sems//e ma'o bya byed lhun gyis grub//de bzhin nyid la phyag 'tshal bstod//bcom ldan 'das nyid byang chub sems//byang chub sems 
89a5(rnying rgyud): nyid thugs kyi mchog/bdag cag rig 'dzin tshogs ba rnams//byang chub sems nyid smon pa dang //'jug dang ldang dang gnyis med de//rang gi byang sems g.yo bar gyis//reg par bgyi zhing nye bar bgyi//nyams su myong bar 'tshal lags na//thugs rje 
89a6(rnying rgyud): che dgongs byin gyis rlobs//bde bar gshegs pas byin gyis rlobs//de skad tshig tu gsol btab pas//bcom ldan snying po 'di skad gsung //sna tshogs rnam dag ngo bo nyid//chos rnams 'phro ba'i tshogs rnams kun//rang bzhin brjod bral rtog pa med//
89a7(rnying rgyud): rang gsang rtogs pa'i dka' ba'i don//srid gsum ye nas rnam dag chos//bdag rtog 'khrul pa'i nyes dmigs kyis//dus ring 'khyams 'gyur 'gro ba rnams//rang rtog dngos po'i las la spyod//log pa'i sems kyis kun brtags kyang //
89b1(rnying rgyud): rang bzhin nyid la 'gyur ba med//ye nas don gyi sangs rgyas la//'khrul pa'i bde sdug 'khor los 'khyams//log rtog dngos med byang chub sems//'khrul rtog mi gnas rgyan gyi mchog/tha ba'i gnas nyid byang chub zhing //srid dngos sangs rgyas 
89b2(rnying rgyud): mngon rdzogs sku//'dzin rtog byang chub snying por gsal//brtan g.yo dngos po thams cad dang //bde chen sku las gzhan med do//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dgongs pa bla na med pa'i gsang ba dpal rta mchog rol pa'i rgyud chen po las de bzhin 
89b3(rnying rgyud): nyid kyi le'u ste lnga pa'o////bcom ldan rgyal ba thams cad kyi//dkyil 'khor snying po bsdus pa'i don//de nas gsang ba'i bdag po yis//dkyil 'khor rnam par 'phro ba'i don//mtshan nyid 'phro zhing rtogs pa dag/rgyal bas bshad phyir 
89b4(rnying rgyud): bskul 'debs mdzad//bcom ldan rgyal ba thams chad kyis//dkyil 'khor snying po brdzus pa'i don//rgya che mdzad pa'i mthu ldan pa//rdo rje gsang ba gsum gyi gnas//rab rgyas mtshan bzhin cho ga'o//yan lag bdag la bstan du gsol//de 
89b5(rnying rgyud): nas mgon po gsang ba'i bdag/gnyis med ye shes la 'gro ba'i//ting nge 'dzin la snyoms zhugs gnas//rgya cher sprul pa'i don bstan mdzad//stong nyid ye shes las byung ba'i//ri bo rgyud 'dzin rigs mtshan can//dbyangs kyi yi ge'i tshul 'jug 
89b6(rnying rgyud): pa'i//rta bdun 'od sogs gsal byed ldan//skar ma'i tshogs dang 'dra ba las//mnyam nyid 'od kyi gong bu'i gzugs//snang ba'i 'od zer lnga yi tshul//phyag rgya mtshan ma sngags dang bcas//rnam 'phros rang rig zhing khams spyod//de dag gong bu'i gzugs 
89b7(rnying rgyud): mtshungs par//gtso bo rdo rje 'dzin pa'i sku//stong nyid snying rje chen po yis//ma lus rgyud rnams gsal mdzad nas//nam mkha' dang ba'i dkyil 'khor bzhin//thugs rje chen po skyon skyong mdzad//sa bon gsang ba'i las byung ba'i//mkha' snang gsal ba kun khyab 
90a1(rnying rgyud): @#//las//sna tshogs kun nas so so nyid//'byung bzhi'i dkyil 'khor rim bzhin bkod//kun 'phrul sa bon las bskyed pa'i//ye shes rin chen sna tshogs gzugs//shar du do shal phreng ba dang //gdugs dang rgyal mtshan rnga yab sogs//
90a2(rnying rgyud): me long rol mo'i tshogs bsam yas//'khor lo rin chen rdo rje pad//twa ra na bzhi rgyan mdzes pa//sgra dbyangs sna tshogs nyi zla'i tshogs//tsakra bar khyams phreng ba dang //dam pa'i 'dod yon lta mo 'khrigs//rin chen 'bar ba'i khri brtsegs gnas//
90a3(rnying rgyud): nyi zla pad+ma'i rin chen rgyan//gnas rnams khyab par ci bzhin par//dkyil 'khor lhun gyis grub pa'o//a A/i I/u U/e ai/o au/oM aH/r-i r-I/l-i l-I/ka kha ga g+ha da/tsa tsha dza dz+ha nya/Ta Tha Da D+ha Na/ta tha da d+ha na/pa pha ba b+ha ma/ya ra la wa/sha Sha 
90a4(rnying rgyud): sa ha k+Sha/de bzhin nyid dbyings rmad 'byung ba'i//bder gshegs kun gyi thugs gsal ba//byin brlabs ya mtshan sprul pa'i sku//dkyil 'khor sna tshogs kun gyi gnas//sems can kun gyi sems chen po//rang bzhin dag pa dri ma med//phung po 
90a5(rnying rgyud): chos rnams sangs rgyas te//'byung lnga dbyings phyug yum gyi mkha'//khams dang skye mched sems dpa' dang //sems ma'i tshogs dang khro mo'i sku//phyi nang srid gsum dngos snang ba'i//dkyil 'khor rigs kyi bdag nyid che//gzugs phung rnam par snang 
90a6(rnying rgyud): mdzad che//tshor ba rin chen 'byung ldan sku//'du shes snang ba mtha' yas sku//'du byed don yod grub pa yin//rnam shes rdo rje sems dpa' ste//thams cad bder gshegs lnga yi sku//sa khams sangs rgyas spyan yin te//chu khams lha 
90a7(rnying rgyud): mo mA ma kI//me khams na bza' dkar mo ste//rlung khams dam tshig sgrol ma'o//nam mkha' kun tu bzang mo'i sku//'byung ba'i dkyil 'khor rnam dag pa'o//dbang po'i spyod yul ma lus kun//thams cad bder gsheg rigs kyi 
90b1(rnying rgyud): sku||mig la rnam par snang mdzad de//rna ba rin chen 'byung ldan che//sna la snang ba mtha' yas nyid//lce la don yod grub pa'i sku//lus nyid rdo rje sems dpa' yin//de ltar snang ba'i dkyil 'khor nyid//ye nas lhun grub gnas bsan//rnal 'byor 
90b2(rnying rgyud): gsang ba don shes bya//phyi rol don shes yul drug 'di//yum chen lnga yi phyag rgya'i dbyibs//gzugs khams sangs rgyas spyan ma ste//sgra tshogs lha mo mA ma kI//dri tshogs na bza' dkar mo'i dbyings//ro drug lha mo sgrol ma yin//reg tshogs lha mo kun 
90b3(rnying rgyud): du bzang me'i sku//dus gsum mnyam sbyor 'du 'bral med//rdo rje pad+mo mnyam sbyor bas//rang rig dkyil 'khor gsal bar mdzod//thams cad ma lus sku gsung thugs//yon tan phrin las rol ba'i rgyan//nga nyid 'du 'bral med pa'i dbyings//thabs 
90b4(rnying rgyud): dang shes rab rol ba'i mchog/sa yi khyad par ma lus pa'ang //rdo rje chos kyis sbyor mdzad dang //yon tan phrin las mthar phyin pas//tshe 'di nyid kyi go 'phang mchog/rdo rje gsang gsum dam tshig che//rang gi mthu yi sbyor byed pa//rtag tu mnyam 
90b5(rnying rgyud): bzhag sbyor ba yis//dkyil 'khor chen po'i gnas bzhugs te//dri med byang chub sems kyi tshul//'gro rnams tshogs bcas dag par byas//rtag tu gsang sngags brjod byas shing //rdo rje mgon po'i sgo gsal brtan//rtag tu gtor sbyin gtang byas te//
90b6(rnying rgyud): khams kyi 'dzin pa med par grol//rtag tu lha la mchod byas te//rang gi bdud rtsis mchod cing rol//rtag tu mi gnyis bstod bya ba//rnam grol thams cad brag ca'i sgra//pha rol phyin bcu dus gcig tu//pad+ma cho ga rtogs par 'gyur//zag pa zad pas sku lnga 
90b7(rnying rgyud): dang //pad+ma'i rigs kun mnyam par sbyor//pad+ma'i dam tshig la gnas pa//gnas kun dkyil 'khor gsal bar 'gyur//pad+ma rang gi gnas su bsgom//'dod chags chos kun spyod pa yi//mgon po rang rang gnas na gsal//dri med dag pa'i 
91a1(rnying rgyud): @#//thig le las//'od zer lnga yi snang ba bcas//rang gi gnas nas 'phro mdzad cing //de nyid mgon po'i sku 'dod gzugs//kun tu pad+ma chags med pas//gnas kun dkyil 'khor gsal bar 'gyur//thub pa chen po'i mtshan 
91a2(rnying rgyud): rnams dang //dpe byad rdzogs par gyur nas ni//yon tan ma lus 'byung ba'i gnas//kun du dregs pa'i sangs rgyas mchog/rdo rje dam tshig la gnas pas//rdo rje rang gi gnas su bsgom//'od 'phros 'dus pa'i gnas phyag rgya'i//ye shes 
91a3(rnying rgyud): mnyam nyid kun don mdzad//thugs rjes rnam mang yul bral zhing //'joms par mdzad pa'i khro bo'i sku//rang gi dkyil 'khor dri bral ba//mngon sum gsang ba'i ye shes sku//'khor lo 'di ma tshig la gnas pas//'khor lo rang gi gnas su 
91a4(rnying rgyud): bsgom//rgyal ba kun gyi rin po che//'phro 'du nyi shu rtsa lnga'i tshogs//phung tshogs khams rnams rnam dag cing //phrin las ye shes bla na med//byang chub dag pa'i cha lugs gnas//dkyil 'khor sprul pa'i byin brlabs sku//dga' ba'i thabs dang 
91a5(rnying rgyud): shes rab sbyor//phyag rgya chen po'i rigs 'dzin gnas//rnal 'byor dad brtson ting 'dzin ldan//'grub 'gyur ring por thogs med spyod//ces gsungs pas 'khor gyi byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs rnams mnyes par gyur to//de bzhin gshegs 
91a6(rnying rgyud): pa thams cad kyi dgongs pa bla na med pa'i gsang ba dpal rta mchog rol ba'i rgyud las ting nge 'dzin gyi le'u ste drug pa'o////de nas byang chub sems dpa' phyag na rdo rje stan las langs te/dga' ba'i mchod pa dang gar thabs kyis dkyil 'khor thams cad mnyes 
91a7(rnying rgyud): par byas nas/bcom ldan 'das la phyag 'tshal te gsol ba btab bo//kye ma rgyal ba kun gyi gtso//sangs rgyas tshogs rnams bskyed pa'i yab//khyed kyis dkyil 'khor sprul mdzad nas//gdul bya'i lam 'di grol byas na//phyi yi 'gro ba ci ltar 
91b1(rnying rgyud): gzung //gang dag dbang po ma smin pa'i//dkar po chos rnams mthong mi 'gyur//de dag thugs rje chen po yis//bzung zhing las la gzhug par snang //ma 'ongs 'gro ba la la dag/'di ltar dkyil 'khor mthong ba rnams//ji ltar dkyil 'khor brtan par 'dul//
91b2(rnying rgyud): zhes gsol ba dang /bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu ma 'ongs pa'i 'gro ba theg pa gsang sngags kyi lam du 'jug par 'dod pa dag gis ni/rdo rje slob dpon la brten te gzugs brnyan gyi dkyil 'khor du gzhug par bya'o//rdo rje slob dpon blo 
91b3(rnying rgyud): gros mchog/dad brtson shes rab stobs dang ldan//bdag chos ma lus shes 'gyur nas//skyob pa byang sems sdom pa blangs//byang sems snying rje chen po dang //dga' ba btang snyoms bzhi bzhag nas//sems can thams cad mgron du gnyer//slar 
91b4(rnying rgyud): nyid byang chub lam la dgod//pha rol phyin pa drug rnams dang //bsdu ba bzhi la legs gnas pas//theg pa chen po'i lam gnyer zhing //nyan thos la sogs blos pa med pa'i blos//gsang ba'i theg pa don gnyer zhing //dkyil 'khor dbang thob dam 
91b5(rnying rgyud): tshig ldan//gsang ba'i rgyud rnams don shes shing //de nyid bcu las goms gyur pas//rang gi ye shes rnam grol zhing //dngos po de nyid brtan par gnas//mu stegs la sogs mi bskyod cing //theg dman la sogs 'gyur ba med//rtag tu 'gro ba bzung ba'i 
91b6(rnying rgyud): blos//dkyil 'khor la sogs las la brtson//mig nas mya ngan 'das pa yi//grong khyer chen por 'gro byed dpon//'jig rten drag brtson chos brgyad dag/btang snyoms spong len med pa nyid//dmus long tshogs kyi mig pu bas//bla ma'i gtso 
91b7(rnying rgyud): bor gzung bar bya//'jug pa'i gang zag phun sum tshogs//zhi des gsod por smra ba dang //'jam zhing 'gro ba'i yid 'ong ba//rtse gcig dad pa brtson 'gyur byed//blo gros yon tan kun la 'jug/lha dang bla ma la sogs gus//
92a1(rnying rgyud): @#//theg pa chen po'i lam dag pas//ngan pa'i tshogs spangs gzhan du gnas//de ni blo gros bzang po yon tan ldan//theg chen gsang ba 'dzin pa'i snod//sa mchog shin tu dben pa'i gnas//ri bo nags tshal 'bras bu dang //me tog chu 
92a2(rnying rgyud): rgyun phun sum tshogs//gnod byed skye bo tshogs bral ba//rnal 'byor grogs bcas dga' 'gyur ba'i//bdag dbang bar chad med pa'i mchog/bsnyen pa ji bzhin rdzogs pa yis//dkyil 'khor bri sogs las rnams bya//phun sum tshogs pa bdun ldan 
92a3(rnying rgyud): pas//bdag gnas sa gzhi'i grogs bcas dang //mchod rdzas yo byad ma lus pa//bsrung zhing byin gyis brlab par bya//rdo rjes gzhir ba dang //gur dang bla re bcas pa yis//khro bo me 'od mtshon cha'i tshogs//bsrung zhing 'khor lo rim bzhin bsgom 
92a4(rnying rgyud): sems can thams cad bsgral ba'i blos//bsod nams tshogs kyis dag par bya//phyag 'tshal mchod pa dbul ba dang //sdig bshags bsod nams rjes yi rang //sangs rgyas mya ngan mi 'da' zhing //chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba dang //lus dbul 
92a5(rnying rgyud): byang sems bskyed pa dang //lam gnas dge ba'i rtsa ba bsngo//rgya cher yongs su bsngo byas shing //sems can don du smon lam gdab//dmigs med pa yi rang bzhin gyis//de dag brtson bral nam mkha' bzhin//rnam thar gsum las 'das pa yi//de 
92a6(rnying rgyud): bzhin nyid kyi ye shes che//thams cad nam mkha'i mtshan nyid de//nam mkha' la yang mtshan nyid med//nam mkha' 'dra ba rnal 'byor pas//don kun mnyam pa nyid la gsal//de la rnam par 'phrul pa'i tshogs//dkyil 'khor chen po sprul byas pas//
92a7(rnying rgyud): rigs kyi zhing khams spyod byed cing //rnal 'byor chen po las kun 'grub//sa phyogs dri med yid 'ong ba'i//grong ngam grong khyer dgon pa'am//ri phugs nags tshal gang rung bar//nyams dga' yan lag brtag par bya//gang du bzang ni 'bras bu bcas//
92b1(rnying rgyud): ne'u gsing chu 'gram me tog bcas//dpal rdzogs yon tan 'byung ba'i gnas//de ni rab gus brtag par bya//nam mkha' 'khor lo dra ba dang //sa gzhi pad+ma dra ba dang //bkra shis phun sum sna tshogs gzhi//bar chad med pa'i mtshan nyid yin//
92b2(rnying rgyud): sa gzhi zho ma rdib med pa yi//da dus su mtho ba phyag rgya che//shar dang byang du gzhol ba ni//phrin las rnam bzhi'i dngos grub 'gyur//lho dang nub tu gzhol bas ni//'jig rten pa yi dngos grub 'gyur//nub dang byang du gzhol ba ni //ting 'dzin 
92b3(rnying rgyud): phro mo'i las gzhan 'gyur//gang zhig shar phyogs mtho ba dang //dbus su dma' ba spang bar bya//sa de bus mo nub tsam du//brkos te slar yang dgang bar bya//mang 'phel gyur na dngos grub mchog/gang bar gyur pa shis pa yin//dma' zhing ma 
92b4(rnying rgyud): legs gyur pa yin//sol du ma rus sogs byung na yang //yang na chu yis dgang byas te//slob dpon rdo rje dril 'dzin pas//mnyam bzhag lha yi sngags brjod cing //shar du mi drag gom pa brgyad//song ste slar ldog blta bar bya//
92b5(rnying rgyud): 'grib pa med na dngos grub 'gyur//snang dang mi snang sa yi bdag/mgu byas rin gyis nyo ba dang //blangs te bar chad med pa'i phyir//bgegs gzhan phra mo bskrad par bya//bar chad med pa'i sa dbus su//rigs kyi bdag po 'byor ldan pas//chos go 
92b6(rnying rgyud): la sogs rgyan thogs shing //pad+ma'i dkyil 'khor legs bzhugs te//'khor los sgyur rgyal cha lugs kyis//mchod sogs sbyar zhing byin brlabs te//rdo rje bsrung ba'i phyag rgya yis//phyogs bcu'i dkyil 'khor rig par bcas//sa yi lha mo srung 
92b7(rnying rgyud): ma rnams//spyan drangs mchod pas mnyes par bya//khyad par sa yi bdag po dang //'gro drug bgegs rnams ma lus kun//dgug cing bu yon mchod sbyin gtang //dri zhim chu yis kun gtor zhing //sngags dang phyag rgyas spang bar bya//gang zhig dngos 
93a1(rnying rgyud): @#//grub la sogs 'dod//rab 'bring tha ma grub don du//lto 'phye brtag pa'i cho ga bzhin//lan lag kun dbang rgya yang bya//de nas dkyil 'khor rgyar mnyam pa'i//sa gzhi khru la sogs tshad du//brkos te sa yi skyon bsal zhing //sa nA ga 
93a2(rnying rgyud): mdog bzang 'jam phyin pas//gang zhig dri zhim chur bcas pa'i//sngags btab gang zhig 'thas par bcag/bsgrub pa'i dkyil 'khor khru dor bstod//rgyar ni khru drug pa yi tshad//tshogs bsag khru gang 'phang du bstod//mtha' skor khru gang pa yi tshad//
93a3(rnying rgyud): dbang bskur sor bzhi'i tshad bstod de//ji ltar gru bzhi rgyar bskyed cing //dbyibs legs me long bzhin du gnas//tshad mnyam grog sbur la sogs pa//rin chen lnga dang sman lnga dang //snying po lnga dang 'bru lnga dag/dri chu sngags kyis btab byas nas//
93a4(rnying rgyud): dkyil 'khor snying por gzhag par bya//phyogs kun mnyam pa nyid dmigs te//stong pa nyid kyi sngags kyis sbyang //dri zhim sngags kyis byin brlabs pa'i//chu rgyun phyogs bcur gtor byas shing //sna tshogs gtor mas mnyes byas te//lha tshogs 
93a5(rnying rgyud): mgron rnams tshim par bya//slob dpon rigs kyi gtsor gnas pa'i//rgyan dang cha lugs rdzogs byas nas//sgrub pa'i grogs mchog blo gros ldan//tshul bzhin du ni gsol ba btab//dbus kyi sa yi char gnas pa//kun 'byung nam mkha' dag pa'i 
93a6(rnying rgyud): sngags//brjod cing 'byung ba'i sa bon las//dkyil 'khor ri brtsegs bsgom par bya//rdo rje bsdams pa sar gzhag cing //byams pa'i rdo rje rnam dag cing //rdo rje dkyil 'khor thugs dang bcas//phyag mtshan dran pa thog mthar rgyan//rdo rje sa gzhi 
93a7(rnying rgyud): rab dang //gur sogs khro bo me 'od bcas//gtso bo gar gyi dbang phyug skus//pad bcas thal mo lan gsum brdab//'od 'phros gnas kun g.yos pa la//lha mo gser mdog rgyan gyis mdzes//me tog phreng ba bum thogs pa'i//lus phyed 
93b1(rnying rgyud): sa las 'thon byas te//mthun na bka' nyan tshul du gnas//mchod bstod zhal bsil sogs 'bul zhing //dri zhim las rnams ma rdzogs par//de srid mi 'gyur brtan bzhugs nas//bdud sogs bsgo ba'i dag bcas mdzad//skyob pa sangs rgyas 
93b2(rnying rgyud): thams cad kyi//spyod pa'i tshul dang khyad par gyis//sa dang pha rol phyin pa las//lha mo khyod ni dpang du shog/ji ltar rgyal ba thams cad kyis//bdud kyi sde rnams 'joms mdzad pa//de bzhin bdag gis bdud las kyang //rgyal bar byas pa'i dkyil 
93b3(rnying rgyud): 'khor brtsam//de nas slob dpon rgyan rdzogs pa'i//rdo rje dri la 'gying bcas pa la//dam tshig nye bar bskul ba yi//glu dag phyogs bzhi'i gnas nas gdon//rgyal bas 'gro ba'i mgon gyur pa'i//chos kyi me long gsal ba ston//bder gshegs 
93b4(rnying rgyud): rnams kyi gsang ba pa//rdo rje gsang gsum dam tshig mchog/ye shes dri ma med pa'i tshul//sgrib sogs ma lus bral byed pa'i//rdo rje pad+mo mnyam pa'i dbyings//dkyil 'khor chen po drug cu'i bdag/theg chen chos kyi 'khor lo'i mdzod//gsang gsum 
93b5(rnying rgyud): pad+mo dag pa'i dbyings//so sor gsal ba'i don bstan pa//rdo rje lta bus gnas kun sbyor//zab cing rgya chen sgra sna tshogs//chos kyi 'khor lo bshad pa'i dus//rgyal po yon tan dpe dang bral//rin chen lhun po'i mdzod mnga' ba//nyams dga' thugs rje'i 
93b6(rnying rgyud): zhing rnams dgongs//bdag med mnyam pa'i ye shes can//phrin las dag pas zad par mdzod//gsang gsum sna tshogs rdo rje'i bdag/rigs mchog shes rab brtson byed cing //spyan pa zang dag pas 'grol gi zags//slar nyid khro bo chen po'i sku//zhal lnga phyag 
93b7(rnying rgyud): sna tshogs 'dzin//'jigs byed 'bar ba'i skur gnas te//mgur nas hUM bsgoms sgra sgrogs shing //rdo rje pad+ma lta ba yis//lha rnams bdag la bsdu byed cing //ma dang ta las byung ba yi//mig gnyis nyi zla'i dkyil 'khor brjid//'bar zhing thams cad dag byas nas//
94a1(rnying rgyud): @#//slar nyid rang gi dkyil 'khor rdzogs//shangs kyi rlung sgom 'bar ba nas//'jig rten thams cad 'bar byed pa//rdo rje'i 'gros las byung ba yi//sna tshogs sa gzhir brtan par byas//oM sum+b+ha ni sum+b+ha hU~M/gr-ih+Na gr-ih+Na hU~M/gr-ih+NA ba ya gr-ih+NA ba ya hU~M 
94a2(rnying rgyud): hU~M/A na ya ho g+ha ga bA na bi d+yA rA dza la hU~M hU~M phaT/phyag gi mtshan ma kun 'khrol zhing //khro bo'i dkyil 'khor spro bar byed//hU~M lha dbang rlung lha yi tshogs//gnod sbyin dri za srin po dang //sha za gshin rje ma mo'i tshogs//mkha' 'gro gnod sbyin ma lus 
94a3(rnying rgyud): rnams//gang dag sa gzhi 'dir gnas pa//srung byed sa yig dag por bas//thos pa tsam gyis myur bar dengs//nga yi bka' las 'da' ma byed//gang zhig gnas pa'i mthu bo che//pad+ma rdo rje kha T+wAM ga/thal ba'i tshogs bzhin rlag par byed//
94a4(rnying rgyud): rab 'bar khro bo'i gnas 'joms pa'o//pad+ma'i 'bar ba'i gzugs 'di yis//gal te gang gi dam tshig bcag/'dod gsum skye gnas gsal byas te//'jigs par 'gyur zhing gzhan 'dis med//sa las yongs su bskong byas te//bgegs 
94a5(rnying rgyud): byed bya phyir phur bar gnas//oM g+ha ta ya g+ha ta ya sarba duSh+Tan phaT/hU~M hU~M hU~M badzra kI la ya/badzra d+ha rod A dz+nyA ba ya ti kA ya gA ka tsit+ta badzra kI la ya hU~M phaT/lag pa bskyod cing rkang pa bskyod//ngo mtshar khro bo'i zur mig bzhin//smin ma khro gnyer g.yon mdzad cing //
94a6(rnying rgyud): kha yi pad+ma 'dzum bcas pas//so so'i phyag rgya gnas su bsgyur//'gying bag bcas pas zhal la bstod//rta mchog khro bo'i gar thabs kyis//zhi rgyas dbang dang drag po'i tshul//kha yi rnam lnga dri bsres chus//dkyil 'khor sa gzhi nyer byugs nas//lha 
94a7(rnying rgyud): rnams gnas su dri yi chus//thig le gru bzhir zlum por bya//rang gi sngags dang me tog rnams//so so'i gnas su dgram par bya//dbu rgyan rol mo mchod pa'i tshogs//lha mgon gnas kyi nye 'khor mdzes//dkyil 'khor lha rnams nye ba'i sa//
94b1(rnying rgyud): mchod cing bstod pas mnyes par bya//cho ga'i yan lag rdzogs pa dang //bzod gsol bsrang ba'i don ched du//rdo rje dril 'dzin dpa' bo yis//phyogs rnams kun tu bskor ba bya//oM badzra ga tas ma ya/ma nu pA la ya/badzra sa twa twe no ba tiSh+Tha dI D+h+yo ye b+ha ba/su po    
94b2(rnying rgyud): Sh+yo me b+ha ba/a nu ra k+to me b+ha ba/su to Sh+yo me b+ha ba/sarba si Sh+Ti me bra yats+tsha/sarba karma su tsa me/tsit+taM shri ya ku ru hU~M/ha ra ha ha hoH/b+ha ga wAn/sarba ta thA ga ta/badzra mA me muny+tsa/badzrI b+ha ba ma hA sa ma ya satwa AH/gsang sngags rgyal po bla med pa'i//
