94b4(rnying rgyud): par bya//oM bder gshegs stobs bcu brtson ldan 'gro ba'i mgon//mi 'jigs la sogs yon tan rtsal med ldan//rigs gnyis thabs kyis brgyud pa'i dkyil 'khor che//bsnyengs pa mi mnga' thugs rje shugs chen po//dga' mdzad   
94b5(rnying rgyud): dbang gi dkyil 'khor tshogs chen la//tshul khrims la sogs phung po mnyes gyur cig/pad+ma gar gyi dbang phyug gtsor byas pa'i//dri bral thugs rje'i dkyil 'khor 'di nyid ni//mnyes pa'i tshogs rnams kun las rnam grol zhing //
94b6(rnying rgyud): bde legs yon tan thams cad 'byung ba'i gnas//'dod yon 'bras bu phun sum tshogs bsgrub dang //'gro rnams ma lus thugs rjes bzung byed cing //khyed rnams la yang mchod pa bya ba'i phyir//dkyil 'khor rnam dag rgya chen rtsom   
94b7(rnying rgyud): lags na//mgon po'i bka' drin rjes su brtse dgongs te//khyed kyis rab tu gnas par mdzad 'os lags//me tog dri bzangs phreng sogs 'di bzhes la//ji srid 'gro ba'i don rnams ma grub bar//de srid thugs rjes bzung zhing bzhugs     
95a1(rnying rgyud): @#//nas ni//bdag gi gnas sogs 'di dag byin gyis rlobs//de nas slob ma la bos te/kye rigs kyi bu khyod theg pa'i snod//rdo rje theg pa'i gzhung 'di nyon//dad gus dga' ba'i shugs phyung shog/sngon gyi de bzhin gshegs rnams kyis//
95a2(rnying rgyud): cho ga bzang po 'di bstan bya//mgon po rdzogs sangs rgyas rnams dang //ma byon de bzhin gshegs pa'i tshogs//de bzhin da lta'i sems can rnams//'gro la phan phyir gnas bzhugs te//gsang ba zab mo bla na med//cho ga bzang po 'di spyod pas//skyes 
95a3(rnying rgyud): mchog rang gi gnas nyid du//kun mkhyen ye shes bde chen brnyes//gsang sngags sbyor ba mnyam med par//padam gar dbang dkyil 'khor gyi//bdud tshogs shin tu mi bzad pa'i//dpung rnams ma lus 'joms par mdzad//de phyir kun mkhyen ye shes kyi//blo gros mchog 
95a4(rnying rgyud): 'di khyod kyis kyang //sangs rgyas chos dang dge 'dun la//kun du skyabs su 'gro bar gyis//rnal 'byor mkha' 'gro gsang sngags can//rigs kyi dkyil 'khor so so yi//'khor lo bzhugs pa'i tshogs rnams la//kun nas skyabs su 'gro bar gyis/dpa' 
95a5(rnying rgyud): bo dpa' mo 'khor dang bcas//byang chub sems dpa'i bdag nyid che//khyad par du yang slob dpon la//khyod kyis skyabs su 'gro bar gyis//de nas legs spyod slob ma yis//skyabs 'gro byang sems sdom pa bzung //dkon mchog gsum la phyag 'tshal 
95a6(rnying rgyud): lo//dkon mchog gsum po gzung bar bya//bdag gis sdig pa byas pa dang //bgyid pa rjes su yi rang ba//mgon po'i spyan sngar mchis nas su//de dag thams cad bshags par bgyi//rgyal ba sras bcas 'khor rnams dang //rang rgyal dgra bcom 'gro rnams kyis//
95a7(rnying rgyud): bsgom bzlas la sogs bsod nams la//dad pas rjes su yi rang ngo //sems nyid mngon par sangs rgyas dang //chos kyi 'khor lo skor byed pa'i//tshogs spyod byang chub sems dpa' la//bdag ni rab tu skyabs su mchi//sangs rgyas rnams 'byung 
95b1(rnying rgyud): ci lta bar//skal ldan byang chub sems dpa' yis//byang chub sems mchog dri med pas//'gro ba rgya che tshogs la dmigs//dus gsum bzhugs pa'i 'gro rnams kyis//thugs kyi dkyil 'khor gsal mdzad pa//de bzhin nyid dbyings byang chub sems//
95b2(rnying rgyud): dus gsum dag tu gtang mi bya//rdo rje slob dpon la gus shing //dus kun du ni yon tan bzung //rnal 'byor gsang ba smad mi bya//rdo rje spun la mig bzhin blta//gsang sngags phyag rgya'i rnam pa dag/brtson pas dus gsum dran par bya//de nas shis 
95b3(rnying rgyud): par bya ba'i phyir//rang dang mtshan ma'i yi ge sngags//byin brlabs ye shes bskyed par bya//slar yang bdug pa me tog dang //mar me dri chab la sogs pa'i//rang gi gnas su 'dug cing dbul//bkra shis shing mchog 'o ma can//lo ma me tog 'bras 
95b4(rnying rgyud): bur bcas//rlon pa drang zhing 'bu rjes med//chag gas med pa'i tshems shing ni//sor ni bcu gnyis dri bzangs byug/rtse mo me tog phreng btags pa//sngags zlos dri chus bsang bar bya//rigs kyi mtshon char byin brlabs pas//oM sarba 
95b5(rnying rgyud): pA pa da ha na badzra shi ta ni swAhA/yi ge gsum bcas sngags brjod cing //shar du kha bltas slob ma la//bden pa bdar zhing sbyin bya zhing //des kyang mi smra blang bar bya//gri rtse lta bus lce bzhar zhing //phra mo'i phyogs kyis so brud de//sa gzhi dri yis 
95b6(rnying rgyud): byugs pa ru//phan tshun mi lta dor bar bya//shar la sogs pa'i phyogs kun gyis//zhi sogs dngos grub mtshan ma brtag/gyen thur rab dang 'bring gis lta//mtshams kyis 'jig rten las rnams 'grub//las dag pa yi sngags 
95b7(rnying rgyud): bzlas nas//sbyang zhing byin gyis brlabs byas pa'i//chu gtsang dris sbyang khyor pa gsum//nye reg 'thor ba'i tshul du sbyin//oM hrI bi shud d+ha sarba d+harma pA pa ni tsA sha bi sho d+ha rma sarba ka lpa na a pa na ya hU~M/gtsang ma'i ras la legs 
96a1(rnying rgyud): @#//bka' ba'i//srung skud mdud pa gsum ldan pa'i//yi ge gsum gyi sngags bzlas nas//de yi dpung pa g.yon par bcing //ku sha rtse mo ma 'khrugs pa'i//bam po gnyis su spams pa yis//yab yum gnyis su byin brlabs pa'i//sngas drang 
96a2(rnying rgyud): stan du sbyin par bya//oM sarba du sti ku ru swA hA//dga' ba'i gtam gyis gzengs bstod cing //mchod pa gtor ma'i tshogs bcas pas//lha tshogs rnams la bsngo byas nas//bden pa brjod cing ci bder nyal//mi smra dge ba'i sems bcas pas//rmi lam mtshan ma brtag 
96a3(rnying rgyud): par bya//slar nyid slob dpon nang song ste//dkyil 'khor gsal ba'i ting 'dzin gyis//lha rnams mchod cing bstod bya ste//dge ba'i sems kyis nyal bar bya//de nas skya rengs shar dus su//slob dpon slob ma sngas 'ongs te//kye kye bzhin bzangs rnams longs

96b1(rnying rgyud): dkyil 'khor gsal byas nas//ye shes lha dang gnyis med pa'i//thabs dang shes rab 'brel byas pas//dkyil 'khor sa la thig gdab bya//shes rab spyan mdzes bcu drug lon pa'am//ji bzhin byin gyis brlabs nas kyang //dkyil 'khor bsgrub pa gnas

97b5(rnying rgyud): gi kha dog tshon bcas bzhag/de yi shar phyogs re'u mig la//tshe mtha' yas dang chos dbang phyug/chags med 'od dang spyan dga' ba'i//pad brtsegs pad+ma'i phreng ma dang //bdag nas rin chen glegs bam rin chen phreng //rang mdog sngags kyis brgyan pa 
97b6(rnying rgyud): bzhag/shar kyi dkyil 'khor dbus gnas par//bde bar gshegs pa mi bskyod pa//rdo rje dril bu'i sngags bcas pas//rang gi 'od bcas gdan la bzhag/de yi shar phyogs de'u mig la//'brug sgra dri ma med pa'i rigs//mngon par snang mdzad bstan 
97b7(rnying rgyud): dbang phyug/chags shing rin chen pad spungs pa//rin po che yi dra ba ste//rim bzhin rang mdog sngags kyis brgyan//lho yi dkyil 'khor dbus gnas ba//bder gshegs rin po che sbyangs kyis//rin chen brtsegs dang dril bu la//rang mdog sngags kyis mtshan 
98a1(rnying rgyud): @#//byas pa'o//de yi shar phyogs re'u mig la//nam mkha'i mdzod dang dra ba can//dkon mchog rgyal mtshan rin chen tog/rin chen gling bu rin chen phreng //rin chen rgyal mtshan rin chen bum//rang gi 'od bcas sngags kyis brgyan//nub kyi 
98a2(rnying rgyud): dkyil 'khor dbus gnas pa//bder gshegs kun du snang ba'i 'od//'khor lo dril bu mtshan ma la//rang 'od sngags kyis mtshan pa bri//pad+mo rigs kyi 'khor lo'i tshad//byang gi dkyil 'khor dbus gnas ba//bder gshegs don yod grub

98a4(rnying rgyud): 'od 'phros sngags kyis mtshan byas pa'i//mtshams kyi re'u mig bzhi rnams la//'od 'phros pad+ma 'dzin pa dang //pad+ma gnon dang bde skyong dang //me long rin chen phreng ba dang //pi wang gar gyi phyag rgya la//rang 'od sngags kyi mdzes par bya//phyi rol 
98a5(rnying rgyud): pad+ma'i shar ba rtsams te//rigs kyi byang sems bzhi bzhir gnas//bde ba'i blo gros rgya mtsho'i sgra//gzi mdangs dang ni 'khor lo yi//nam mkha'i mdzod kyi 'khor lo rin po che//pad lhug 'khor lo dbyug pa rnams//rang 'od sngags kyi mtshan mar

175b1(rnying rgyud): gshin rje mnyes//dbyings su mtshan ma shes rab thobs brdungs//thugs rjes sems can phrin las mtshan ma 'joms//dbu mar ye shes snying rje mi rtog dang //mi g.yo mgon po mnyes mdzad mu ha la//rdo rje gtum mo mtshan ma'i dngos po 
175b2(rnying rgyud): bcom//thugs rje rngam tshul srid pa thal bar brlag/gnyis la mi gnas mtha' bral dbu mar bsdigs//ha ya grI ba mnyes mdzad tsaN+Da li//phra mor 'bigs pa'i ye shes dbyings su 'phen//'bigs pa'i ye shes gnad du thugs rjes rdeg/ma 
175b3(rnying rgyud): khyogs dbu ma chen po'i don ston pa//gzhan gyis mi thub mnyes byed sha ya ti//mtshan ma med pa'i dbyings thos rig pa'i sgra//dbyings nyid rig shes dbyings la nyan pa'i sgra//de la bstan pa gsung gi sgra 'byin pa//bdud rtsir 'khyil ba sha ya ya

175b5(rnying rgyud): shugs kyis 'gro ba gnas nas 'dren//gnyis med mchod bskul dbu ma'i dgyes pa stobs//ma ha ha la mnyes mdzad tsun dam//bskyod pa'i ye shes sa rnams dus gcig gcod//thugs rje shugs kyis 'gro ba gnas pas spor//thabs dang shes rab bskyod

175b7(rnying rgyud): za byed bya tha ma//sdang mig mi 'gyur thugs rjes zhi la gnon//mthu rtsal rngom brjod 'gyur med bdud dpung bzlog/tsham dam ral gsigs gdangs dar 'jig rten 'gengs//de phyir 'gyur med phra men tsas ra//rtog pa'i dgra spangs dran pa'i 
176a1(rnying rgyud): @#//dgon par gnas//'khrul skyon dri med shes bzhin rdzi ma tshor spong //'gro don dus la bab na thugs rjes 'jug/de phyir 'jug par byed pa'i ha ri na//chos dbyings gnas ma la rtog pa'i skyon sel zhing //thugs rje spu ris sprul pa bsam mi 
176a2(rnying rgyud): khyab//dug gsum 'joms mdzad sha rus khrag la rol//sel byed sprul pas don mdzad rgya ma//yang dag zon chags 'gro don thabs mkhas ldan//mi 'dul zing thub btang snyoms chen por bzhugs//dus la bab nas brtson 'grus shugs dang ldan//
176a3(rnying rgyud): de'i phyir mkhas brtson mdzad pa'i rtsal ma//ye shes dbyings la 'jug pa shes bya za//dbyings nyid shes la 'jug pa nyon mongs za//gnyis med ye shes phrin las 'khor ba za//de phyir za byed pu ka mu ka ba//rigs btsun gnyen por gyur pas thams cad 'dul//
176a4(rnying rgyud): phrin las rtsal gyis bdud dgud thams cad 'joms//stong nyid sgra bsgrags mu stegs wa tshogs 'jigs//de phyir 'joms byed rtsa mchog sing ha ma//dbyings la ye shes nyal zhing thugs rje rmi//lhid nag nag po drag po sku ston pa//thugs mchog rnam dag chos 
176a5(rnying rgyud): nyid dbyings su mkhyen//de phyir rmi byed phra men ri k+Sha ma//ye shes thugs rje stobs kyis mtha' gnyis gnon//tsham dmar la gsigs sprul pas bdud dpung 'joms//las su ci mdzad brtson 'grus stobs kyis rol//de phyir stongs ldan phra men ha k+Sha 
176a6(rnying rgyud): ma//lhag mthong chos dbyings zhi gnas rlan gyis brgyal//thugs rje 'khor bar smon lam stobs kyis myos//rtog 'joms rdzas la gtsang sme med par rol//de phyir phra men myos byed u ka ra//yid g.yo las can sems par gyur pa ni//
176a7(rnying rgyud): ye shes dbang gis thugs rjes 'gro la g.yo//don mdzad god 'brang ye shes dbyings su mkhyen//de bying tshul khrims chen po kyi kya ma//bsam pa'i dngos po 'dod la 'dun pa ni//dbyings nyid mnyam nyid ye shes mkhyen pa za//thugs rje 'dun 
176b1(rnying rgyud): pas 'khor ba bag chags sbyong //de phyir phra men za byed don ka ma//dbyings la sems 'dzin yid la byed par 'gyur//ye shes dbyings rtogs drangs pa nyin mo nyal//'gro ma dngos rmongs la thugs rjes mtshan mo ldan//ye shes thugs rjes drangs 
176b2(rnying rgyud): pa'i u lu ka//brtags pa'i dngos la rtse gcig ting 'dzin 'gyur//mnyam nyid mtha' bral dbyings las mi g.yo ba//rtog ge spyod yul gyis pas mi mthong ba//de phyir sbed byad phra men pa ya ta//las 'bras bden la yid ches dad gyur te//mnyam nyid dbyings 
176b3(rnying rgyud): su ye shes thugs kyis mgyogs//zhi gnas spyan rdzi lhag mthong bgrod tshags chung //de phyir phra men mgyogs byed b+ha ga shi ta//nges pa'i dngos la 'dzin pa'i mos 'gyur//lhag mthong ye shes dbyings nyid mnyam 'gyur 'dzin//zhi gnas gces par 'dzin pa mi 
176b4(rnying rgyud): 'phrogs pa//de phyir phra men 'dzin ma tsa ta ma//'dris pa'i dngos po ma brjed dran par gyur//dbyings dang ye shes gnyis med ma brjed dran//ting 'dzin 'bum phrag lhag mthong so sor gsal//gnyis la ma rmongs zin ma ti ti la//tshes rnams 'byed pa'i byed par gyur pa 
176b5(rnying rgyud): ni//mnyam nyid ngang du log lta sel byed cing //rig pa skyed pas so ma nyi the tshom gcod//de phyir phra men gsod byed na ku la//'jug par rang dbang med pa'i gnyid gyur pa//bstan pa'i chos sgrogs log lta myos byed cing //mnyam dang mi mthun log lta myos byed phyir//
176b6(rnying rgyud): thams cad myos byed phra men 'u shi ka//gsum 'dus gcod pa'i rig pa gyur pa ni//phrin las bye brag mang po ci mdzad cing //phyag rgya bzhi po ma skyes dbyings su bros//de phyir phra men 'bros byed ka si ka//'khor ba'i rdo rje brjod pas mya ngan 'das//chos sku rdo rje brjod 
176b7(rnying rgyud): pas mtha' gnyis spangs//rjod byed brjod bral don gyi tshig dang bral//de phyir rjod byed badzra ba sha ma//chos nyid rdo rjes mtshan ma'i dngos po bshig/ye shes rdo rjes gzung 'dzin rtog pa bsregs//phyag mtshan rdo rjes dregs pa'i lha bdud bcom//de phyir 'joms byed

178a6(rnying rgyud): snyigs ma lnga ldan lnga brgya'i tha mal//srid gsum 'khor ba'i sems can gdul dka' ba//gsad pa rnyed la las kyis 'dul bar mdzad//sdug bsngal dbyings su gsol mdzad mar na//gzi brjid can de me yi khams te mchog/'bar ba mchog gis gdug 
178a7(rnying rgyud): pa ma lus bsregs//za byed bskal pa dus mtha'i tshul ston pa//lhag mthong ye shes 'bar 'phro lag na ye//mche ba dag pas skye bzhi rtsad gcod bzhugs//nyan thos tha ba zlog mdzad pag gi dbu//'og gi phyogs nas srid gsum stobs kyis spor//
178b1(rnying rgyud): skye bzhi rtsad gcod za byed par ti//'chi bdag thams cad gnas nas ma 'phos mkhyen//thams cad gsod byed dam nyams 'chi bdag mdzad//dam tshig bsrung ba'i 'chi bdag zlog mdzad cing //lung gi dur khrod dbang mdzad dza mun ti//'jigs las lhags pa'i 
178b2(rnying rgyud): grags ni gzhan med mkhyen//stong phrag brgyad cu grags la mda' mdzad cing //sna chen glang sna bdud rtsi ge'u wang mthong //sdug bsngal nad gsos mdzad pa'i bu tam//chu las lhag pa'i nor ni gzhan med mkhyen//nor gyi tshogs rnams ma lus 'phrog mdzad

178b7(rnying rgyud): gsung dang thugs rdo rje las thabs dang shes rab kyi byang chub kyi sems kyi dbang 'di bka' stsal to//dkyil 'khor 'dom gang gru bzhi la dbus su pad+ma 'dab ma brgyad/pad+ma'i lte ba la rab gta'i maN+Dal byas te/dbus su badzra he ru ka/shar du bud 
179a1(rnying rgyud): @#//d+ha he ru ka/lhor rad na he ru ka/nub tu padam he ru ka/byang du karma he ru ka/mtshams bzhir khro mo dbang phyug ma bzhi//de'i phyi rim snam bu la go'u rim brgyad//de'i phyi rim la sing ha ma brgyad//sgo bzhi dang sgo khyud dang bcas gzhing gi rgyal 
179a2(rnying rgyud): mtshan/ras chen gyi 'phan gdugs/tsit+ta'i thog/mi sa'i bla res de nas bcom ldan 'das thabs dang shes rab gnyis su med pa'i byang chub kyi sems yum gyi sku la bstims te/yum kun du bzang mos rang gis mtha' la byang chub kyi sems blangs 
179a3(rnying rgyud): te/nyid las mi gzhan pa'i 'khor rnams dbang bskur ro//de nas bcom ldan 'das nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las phyung ngo//byang chub sems nyid sgom pa'i dbang //dbyings dang dbyings kyi dkyil 'khor 'dir//sems nyid rtogs pa'i thig btab ste//de

180b4(rnying rgyud): so//byang chub sems kyis dang 'dir dbang bskur ro/glang po dpal gyi thugs ka nas//lcags kyi zer chen bzhi 'phros te//glang po dpal chen he ru ka'i//lus ngag yid gsum sku gsung thugs kyi dkyil 'khor las//'od zer dgu 'phros gdug pa ma lus 
180b5(rnying rgyud): bcom//'gro ba mtha' dag rnams kyi don byas te//bdag gi rnam par shes pa'i tshogs brgyad kyang //kun gzhi snying la 'dus pa'i he ru ka//de ltar lags pas rtogs na byang chub sems//gang nas btsal kyang rnyed par mi 'gyur te//byang chub 
180b6(rnying rgyud): sems kyis deng 'dir dbang bskur ro//bdag med khams kyi snod gang du//rgya che gsal ba'i khams bkang ste//re dogs med par phung 'gro yang //nyam nga med par lam gyi mchog/gza' gtad med pas don du bde//de bas rang sems bgrod 
180b7(rnying rgyud): pa'i sangs rgyas byang chub sems ni yongs zhing rgya che ste//de bas bdag gis lus rtogs na//snod bcud la sogs rtogs pa'i skyes mchog rnams//shes gsal blo ldan rnams la ye nas rnyed pa gsal//bla ma'i rgyud mchog de la yod ces gsungs te rdzogs//
181a1(rnying rgyud): @#//rdzogs pa'i byang chub sems kyis dbang bskur ro//bsgoms pas rnyed par mi 'gyur ma bsgoms stong bral med//thugs nas zhal du byung ba 'gro ba'i don du gsungs//rna rgyud ston pas thugs brtol te//lus dang sems las ma gtong 
181a2(rnying rgyud): bsgom du med//bsgoms nas sems nyid gzhan nas btsal yang rnyed par ma gsungs so//dus gsum rgyal bas ye nas ma bcos te//rang sems gdod nas ma skyes chos kyi sku//chos nyid bsgom par bya ba gang yang med//man ngag shes rab 
181a3(rnying rgyud): chen des gsungs pas//lus dang sems las mgatogs na//bdag gi sku gsung thugs la tshang bar gnas//gal te brjod pa'i mtha' bzhi yod na ni//don rdzogs sgom pa po yang yod par gyur//lus sems skye ba med bden pas//bsgom du 
181a4(rnying rgyud): par 'jog pa gang //don kun rdzogs shing chub par der gnas so//byang chub sems kyis deng 'dir dbang bskur ro//zla gsang dkar dmar rlung ka lci//zla ba shes rab gsal ba thabs chen te//thig le byang chub sems nyid dkar la 'tsher ba yi//ngan 
181a5(rnying rgyud): yug gi rak+ta byang chub sems//rlung la shor du mi rtsal thig le sgom du sba//de las ma gtogs sangs rgyas gzhan na med//man ngag tshul du zla ba'i gzugs yin te//gsang ba'i tshul du yid kyi rnam shes nyid kyang zla ba yin//thig le sems kyi 
181a6(rnying rgyud): bzhin snum la 'dri la//man ngag len par byed pas sems kyi thig le yin//sems las ma gtogs sangs rgyas//gzhan na med//byang chub sems kyis nged 'dir dbang bskur ro/rang 'byung che ba'i ye shes mi 'gyur la//chos nyid rang gis 
181a7(rnying rgyud): pa'i rang bzhin la//dmigs bral ting 'dzin rgya mtshor rab sprul te//yid ni 'phags pa'i lam ni gnas pa ni//phra zhing zab pa bstan dka' rgyal ba'i sku//dran pa byed pa'i rtsol dang srog chags gsum//kun gzhi pad+ma'i yig 'brugs la brten 
181b1(rnying rgyud): byang chub sems kyis dang 'dir dbang bskur ro//rgyu ni skye ba med pas rnam par dag/'bras bu 'gags pa med pas byang chub sems//byang chub sems de skye med ye shes dbyings//skye med mi 'gyur mi shigs rdo rje'i don//sems la rdo rje sems dpa' rab

206b5(rnying rgyud): 'phel ba ni//'dzam gling sa stengs khyab par skyes//ma hA ni le zhes bya ba//ming ni tha skar rdo rje yin//me tog tha skar gzhon nu ste//snye ma zur gsum til gang 'dra//rdo rje brag kyang 'joms par nus//sha ri ram dang rus pa yi//sbyar ba'i 'phrad kyi dam par rung //

278b5(rnying rgyud): glud du 'bul//dam rdzas mchod gtor la sogs pa//khyed kyi thugs dam bskang phyir 'bul//srid pa'i bdud rje 'khor bcas kyis//sngon gyi dam tshig rjes dgongs la//ji ltar bcol ba'i las mdzod cig/bdud mo chen mo re ma ti//ma bdud kun gyi sde dpon bzhengs//
278b6(rnying rgyud): dpal ldan nag mo 'jigs gzugs can//nag mo sha zlog bdud kyi ma//sku la mthing nag ber chen gsol//chibs su bong bu grwa dmar chibs//khyod kyi cho 'phrul bstan pa ni//ma bdud 'bum phrag pho nyar 'byed//nag mo stong sde las la 'dzud//snang srid 
278b7(rnying rgyud): bdud mo khyod kyi 'khor//dam tshig dbang gis gnas 'dir bzhugs//cang shes bong bu la sogs pa//mthun pa'i rdzas kyis thugs dam bskang //srid pa'i bdud mo 'khor bcas kyis//ji ltar bcol ba'i phrin las mdzod//ri rab chen po'i lho 
279a1(rnying rgyud): @#//phyogs nas//gshin rje ya ma rA dza ste//gshin rje'i tshogs kyi sde dpon bzhengs//sku mdog nag po ral pa can//gdug pa'i thod sprul g.yang gzhis brgyan//gcan gzan gdug pa'i pags pas dkris//sha za'i mgo gcad 
279a2(rnying rgyud): thod du bcings//zhal bgrad mche ba rab tu gtsigs//ma he nag po chibs su chibs//khyod kyi cho 'phrul bstan pa ni//gshin rje pho mo pho nyar 'gyed//'chi bdag gshin rje btsun mo ste//gsod byed mon mo'i tshul 'dzin cing //gshin rje mo yi sde dpon 
279a3(rnying rgyud): bzhengs//za byed dur khrod chas kyis brgyan//g.yang gzhi nag mo sku la gsol//chu glang nag po chibs su chibs/khyod kyi cho 'phrul bstan pa'i//yi dgas pho mo las la 'dzud//snang srid gshin rje khyod kyi 'khor//dam tshig dbang gi gnas 'dir 
279a4(rnying rgyud): bzhugs//ma he chu glang la sogs pa//mthun pa'i rdzas kyis thugs dam bskang //gshin rje pho mo 'khor dang bcas//ji ltar bcol ba'i phrin las mdzod//ri rab chen po'i nub phyogs nas//srin po nag po glog phreng can//srin po'i tshogs kyi sde 
279a5(rnying rgyud): dpon bzhengs//sku mdog mthing nag 'bar ba la//glog dang 'od kyi phreng bas brgyan//'brug chen 'ur ba chibs su chibs//khyod kyi cho 'phrul ston pa ni//srin po 'bum phrag pho nyar 'gyed//srin mo gsod byed las la 'dzud//gcan gzan gdug pa phyi nas 
279a6(rnying rgyud): rol//'khor du snang srid srin mos bskor//srin mo sbal mgo khrag mig ma//srin mo rnams kyi sde dpon bzhengs//sku mdog dmar nag 'jigs su rung //lus la khrag gi lwa nag gsol//rus sbal dkar po'i thor tshugs can//chibs su rnga mo gdong

279b3(rnying rgyud): chas 'bum sde las la 'dzud//'jig rten gnod sbyin 'khor gyis bskor//gnod sbyin chen mo blo mon ma//gnod sbyin bu mo gdug pa can//nag mo rdo rje lcags mchu ma//snyan cha rin chen ne'u les brgyan//sku la ba ti nag po gsol//chibs su lcags kyi 
279b4(rnying rgyud): phag mo chibs//khyod kyi cho 'phrus bstan pa ni//nor 'dzin mo ni pho nyar 'gyed//blo mon nag mo las la 'dzud//gnod sbyin mo ni 'bum sdes bskor//shan pa gnod sbyin yab yum 'khor//dam tshig dbang gis gnas 'dir bzhugs//stag seng phag mo 
279b5(rnying rgyud): la sogs pa'i//mthun pa'i rdzas kyis thugs dam bskang //gnod sbyin pho mo 'khor dang bcas//ji ltar bcol ba'i phrin las mdzod//ri rab chen po'i steng phyogs su//gza' bdud chen po mi bzad pa//rog ti khyab 'jug rA hu la//gza' 
279b6(rnying rgyud): bdud sde dpon chen po bzhengs//mgo dgu smad sbrul mgo mangs lus//chibs su 'brug chen glog 'gyu chibs//khyod kyi cho 'khrul bstan pa ni//'brug sgra stong gsum ldi ri ri//nam mkha' glog 'od khyugs se khyugs//gza' bran rgyu skar pho 
279b7(rnying rgyud): nyar 'gyed//thog ser ki kang drag tu 'bebs//gza' bdud stong gi 'khor gyis bskor//gnam sman thog gi bu yug ma//gdong mo rnams kyi sde dpon bzhengs//sku mdog dmar skya mdzes pa la//na bza' gu zu mdzes pa gsol//'brug sgra glog 
280a1(rnying rgyud): @#//gis 'od la zhon//khyod kyi cho 'phrul bstan pa ni//gza' chen nyi zla klad la bskor//rgyu skar tshogs ni zhal la 'char//lha sman 'bum ni pho nyar 'gyed//gdong zor ma mo las la 'dzud//zor gdong srin mo'i 'khor gyis bskor//gza' gdong gnam sman 
280a2(rnying rgyud): yab yum 'khor//dam tshig dbang gis gnas 'dir bzhugs//'brug chen bur ba la sogs pa'i//mthun pa'i rdzas kyis thugs dam bskang //gza' bdud gdong mo sman dang bcas//ji ltar bcol ba'i phrin las mdzod//ri rab chen po'i 'og phyogs nas//klu bdud 
280a3(rnying rgyud): nag mo mgo dgu po//klu rnams kun gyi sde dpon yin//sku stong dung kha nag po la//tsha ba'i 'bu srin nyi li yi//sku skyud dug sbrul nag po la//grang ba'i sbal lcong khra la la//chibs su chu srin mgo dgu chibs//khyod kyi cho 'phrul bstun pa ni//klu rgyal stong ni pho nyar 'gyed//klu bran 
280a4(rnying rgyud): 'bum ni las la 'dzud//klu bdud gdug pa'i 'khor gyis bskor//klu mo yak+Sha nag mo ni//klu bdud ma mo'i sde dpon bzhengs//sku mdog dud kha 'jigs bag can//sbrul nag gshibs pa'i ral ba las//mdzo dang shu ba'i tsha cha 'phro//sbrul gyi thul ban bzar 
280a5(rnying rgyud): gsol//sbal sbrul nye'u tha ra ra//rus sbal nag po chibs su chibs//khyod kyi cho 'phru bstan pa ni//klu rigs lnga yi sbrul pa 'gyed//sa bdag gdug pa'i pho nya gtong //snang srid klu mo'i 'khor dang bcas//gdug pa klu bdud yab yum 'khor//
280a6(rnying rgyud): dam tshig dbang gis gnas 'dir bzhugs//chu srin rus sbal la sogs pa'i//mthun pa'i rdzas kyis thugs dam bskang //klu bdud pho mo yab yum 'khor//ji ltar bcol ba'i phrin las mdzod//ri rab chen po mdzes pa yi//bang rim gnyis 
280a7(rnying rgyud): pa'i shar phyogs la//gnod sbyin rgyal po gang ba bzang //srid pa lha yi sde dpon bzhengs//mdog dkar ral ka 'bar ba gsol//gnam ru dkar po chibs su chibs//khyong kyi cho 'phrul bstan pa ni//gnod sbyin stong ni pho nyar 'gyed//
280b1(rnying rgyud): lhad mag 'bum sde las la 'dzud//bden can ni g.yas nas rol//go mtshon can ni g.yon nas rol//gnod skyin lha yi 'khor dang bcas//skyes bu gnam rgyal chen po ni//rgyal phran kun gyi sde dpon bzhengs//na bza' dar zab ber chen gsol//
280b2(rnying rgyud): rta dkar rngog dmar chibs su chibs//khyod kyi cho 'phrul bstan pa ni//rgyal phran stong sde pho nyar 'gyed//grong rgyu phra mo las la 'dzud//the rgyal phra mo'i 'khor dang bcas//spyi bdud rgyal po gu rum rtse//mi ma lha yi sde dpon bzhengs//sku stod rgya che'i 
280b3(rnying rgyud): slag pa gsol//'bum gyi skad kyi sham thabs can//dbu la shel rmog dkar po gsol//chibs su rdo rta gro bo chibs//khyod kyi cho 'phru la ston pa ni//srid pa'i khyung chen klad na lding //stag seng tshad ma phyi nas rol//sde dpon mched lnga pho nyar 'gyed//
280b4(rnying rgyud): ma sangs spun dgu las la 'dzud//lha ma srin gyi 'khor dang bcas//skyes pu dkar po sde gsum yang //dam tshig dbang gis gnas 'dir bzhugs//gnam ru rta dkar glo bo chibs//mthun pa'i rdzas kyis thugs dam bskang //dkar po sde gsum 
280b5(rnying rgyud): 'khor dang bcas//ji ltar btsal ba'i phrin las mdzod//bang rim gnyis pa'i lho phyogs nas//ma chen srid pa'i rgyal mo ni//snang srid ma mo'i sde dpon bzhengs//nab za' dar nag gu zug sol//chibs su 'brug mo yid mgyogs chibs//khyod kyi cho 'phrul 
280b6(rnying rgyud): ston pa ni//tshogs kyi rje mo pho nyar 'gyed//sman mo lnga bcu rtsa brgyad 'khor//snang srid ma mo'i tshogs kyis bskor//e ka dza Ti ral gcig ma//bka' yi srung ma'i sde dpon ni//sku la na bza' 'thing nag gsol//chibs su je rta nag mo chibs//
280b7(rnying rgyud): khyod kyi cho 'phrul ston pa ni//srin mo nag mo pho nyar 'gyed//bal mo nag mo las la 'khol//ma mo nag mo sna tshogs sprul//smug nag 'bum gyi 'khor dang bcas//dpal ldan nag mo mgyogs byed ma//mkha' 'gro tshogs kyi sde 
281a1(rnying rgyud): @#//dpon bzhengs//na bza' mthing nag gu zu gsol//chibs su so bya rog po zhon//khyod kyi cho 'phrul ston pa ni//rigs kyi mkha' 'gro so nyar 'gyed//sna tshogs mkha' 'gro las la 'dzud//snang srid mkha' 'gro'i 'khor gyis 
281a2(rnying rgyud): bskor//dpal ldan nag mo sde gsum yang //dam tshig dbang gis gnas 'dir bzhugs//'brug dang dre rta so bya sogs//mthun pa'i rdzas kyis thugs dam bskang //nag mo sde gsum 'khor dang bcas//ji ltar bcol ba'i phrin las mdzod//bang rim 
281a3(rnying rgyud): gnyis pa'i nub phyogs nas//skyes bu rdo rje bdud 'dul ni//gnod sbyin ru tra'i sde dpon bzhengs//na bza'amathing nag ber chen gsol//ral pa nag po mun ltar gtibs//chibs su rta dre nag po chibs//zhing lpags gyon pa pho nyar 'gyed//
281a4(rnying rgyud): ral chas bgos pa las la 'dzud//snang sring ru tra'i 'khor gyis bskor//btsan rgyal srog zan dmar po ni//srid pa'i btsan gyi sde dpon bzhengs//sku la dar dkar ras dmar gsol//chibs su rta dmar glog 'gyu chibs//khyod kyi cho 'phrul 
281a5(rnying rgyud): ston pa ni//dbu la bse rmog dmar po gsol//btsan po kha bzhi pho nyar 'gyed//mgyogs pa sde brgyad las la 'dzud//snang sred dregs pa'i btsan gyis bskor//skyes bu lam shud dmar po ni//btsan 'gong rnams kyi sde dpon bzhengs//
281a6(rnying rgyud): sku la zab dmar ber chen gsol//chibs su rta dam ra yi dam phyogs chibs//khyod kyi cho 'phru la bstan pa ni//g.yas su bro brdung tshi li li//g.yon na spyugs glu kyu ru ru//rta zhon stod ni phyin 'breng //srid pa'i btsan 'gong 'khor dang 
281a7(rnying rgyud): bcas//skyes mchog dmar po sde gsum ni//dam tshig dbang gis gnas 'dir bzhugs//dri nag rta dmar la sogs pa'i//mthun pa'i rdzas kyis thugs dam bskang //dmar po sde gsum 'khor dang bcas//ji ltar bcol 
281b1(rnying rgyud): pa'i phrin las mdzod//bang rim pa zhi ba'i byang phyogs nas//bi nA ya ka bgegs kyi rgyal//phog 'dren kun gyi sde dpon bzhengs//na bza' mthing nag ber chen gsol//chibs su rta nag mgyogs pa chibs//khyod kyi cho 'phrul ston pa ni//rgyal 
281b2(rnying rgyud): chen bzhi ni pho nyar 'gyed//phyogs skyong tshogs ni las la 'dzud//snang srid bgegs kyi tshogs kyis bskor//dbang phyug bu lang nag po ni//mu stegs tshogs kyi sde dpon bzhengs//na ba za' mthing nag ber chen bsol//chibs su stag 
281b3(rnying rgyud): seng ma he chibs//khyod kyi cho 'khrul bstan pa ni//sa gzhi kun ni dmag gis 'gengs//bar snang mtshon dang dar gyis bkang //nam mkha'i glog dang 'od kyis 'gengs//gcan gzan 'dab chags rgyug cing lding //mu stegs nag 
281b4(rnying rgyud): po'i 'khor gyis skor//dmu bdud nag mo bkrag med ni//dmu bdud tshogs kyi sde dpon bzhengs //na bza' mthing nag per chen gsol//chibs su dmu rta nag po chibs//khyod kyi cho 'phrus bstan pa ni//dmu bdud 'bum phrag pho nyar 'gyed//
281b5(rnying rgyud): dmu bran stod sde las la 'dzud//snang sring dmu yi 'khor gyis bskor//skyes mchog nag po sde gsum yang //dam tshig dbang gis gnas 'dir bzhugs//stag seng ma he rta la sogs//mthun pa'i rdzas kyis thugs dam bskang //phyi rol nag po'i tshogs 
281b6(rnying rgyud): dad bcas//ji ltar bcol ba'i phrin las mdzod//ri rab chen po mdzes pa yi//bang rim gsum pa'i shar phyogs la//tshangs pa dkar po srog gi bdag/lha chen ging gi sde dpon bzhengs//srid pa gsum gyi myur mgyogs par//sku la bya rgod thul ba 
281b7(rnying rgyud): gsol//chibs su seng ge dkar po chibs//khyod kyi chos 'phrul bstan pa ni//ging chen bcwa brgyad pho nyar 'gyed//ging bdud pho mo las la 'gyed//mi rta ra lug bya dang phye//stag dang bya rgod la sogs pa'i//mthun pa'i rdzas kyis thus dam bskang //bang 
282a1(rnying rgyud): @#//rim gsum pa'i lho phyogs la//gnod sbyin rgyal po nor gyi bdag/sku la dom gyi slag pa gsol//chibs su ba glang sna rings chibs//khyod kyi cho 'phrul bstan pa ni//skyes pa brgya dang bud med brgya//nyan thos stong dang sngags pa stong //
282a2(rnying rgyud): 'bag sed mon bu bsam mi khyab//stag seng ba la glang spu dang spre//mthun pa'i rdzas kyis thugs dam bskang //bang rim gsum pa'i nub phyogs la//rdo rje mdzes pa zhes bya ba//'jig rten lha yi sde dpon yin//mthar shel gyi na bza' gsol//
282a3(rnying rgyud): kun kyang shel gyi na bza' gsol/ra dkar gnam ru chibs su chibs//khyod kyi cho 'phru ston pa ni//'dzin ging bzhi bcu pho nyar 'gyed//rang gi 'khor sde las la 'dzud//tsa ra bya khyi la sogs pa'i//mthun pa'i rdzas kyis thus dam bskang //
282a4(rnying rgyud): bang rim gsum pa'i byang phyogs la//lha chen nag po chen po ste//bstan pa'i bsrung ba'i sde dpon bzhengs//na bza' ljang nag ber nag gsol//chibs su bdud rta bra nag chibs//khyod kyi cho 'khrul bstan pa ni//lha dmag phyogs kyi dbugs 'byin

282a7(rnying rgyud): sbas pa'i lha srin sde brgyad dang /gsang ba'i lha srin sde brgyad dang //yang gsang gter gyi lha srin dang //drag po sngags kyi lha srin dang //las byed myur mgyogs lha srin dang //brtags shing chags pa'i lha srin dang //sde brgyad 'khor dang bcas pa la//
282b1(rnying rgyud): khyed kyi thugs dam bskang ba ni//shes ldan mi dang cang shes rta//theg chen glang dang 'dod 'jo'i ba//rin chen lug dang gtsang ldan ra//thos gsar khyi dang mkha' lding bya//khyed kyi bzhed pa'i dkor du 'bul//rin chen rgyan ldan dar

282b3(rnying rgyud): rnams kyi glud du 'bul//dam rdzas sbyar ba'i mchod gtor dang //mthun rdzas ldan pa'i bshos cha dang //snang gsal 'od ldan mar me dang //rgyan ldan mdzes pa'i gling tshad dang //mdzes pa'i gzugs dang snyan pa'i sgra//zhim pa'i dri dang bda' ba'i ro//
282b4(rnying rgyud): khyed kyi thugs dam bskang phyir 'bul//'jig rten dregs pa 'khor dang bcas//sngon gyi dam tshig rjes dgongs te//mdos glud mthun rdzas bzhes pa'i dkor//sna tshogs sbyar ba 'di bzhes la//sangs rgyas bstan pa khyed kyis srungs//dkon mchog

282b6(rnying rgyud): 'chi ba rnam brgyad dang //gshis ngan 'gum pa brgyad cu gcig/gzugs can dgra dang gzugs med bgegs//rmi lam mtshan ltas ngan pa rnams//zhi dang bsal zhing bzlog pa dang //gdul dang tshar gcad las mdzod cig/zhes brjod rang gi mthun rdzas bstabs//bskang 
282b7(rnying rgyud): bzlog gsang dang gsol sogs//ji ltar 'dod pa'i phrin las bcol//gang la dmigs pa de bzhin 'grub//zhes bka' stsal to//'jig rten gyi dregs pa can gyi sde dpon 'khor dang bcas pa rnams mchod bstod rgya chen po'i sgo nas nye bar bsgrub pa'i le'u ste lnga pa'o////
283a1(rnying rgyud): @#//tu bshad bya ba//dregs pa'i 'khor tshogs 'di rnams kun//ngo bo rang rig byang chub sems//ma skyes dbyings las g.yos pa med//'jig rten gnas dang mthun 'jug cing //mchod dang bstod pas mnyes 'gyur bas//'jig rten mchod bstod 
283a2(rnying rgyud): zhes su brjod//phyi nang gsang ba'i bye brag ste//snang dang srid pa'i dkyil 'khor kun//gsang ba'i bdag po de nyid dang //gnas skabs mchod bstod phyi yin no//da sha ba'i ting nge 'dzin las byung //rang gi snang ba 'dus pa'i tshogs//dbang po'i yul gyi longs 
283a3(rnying rgyud): spyod dang //gtsang sgra'i rtog pa sbyang ba'i rdzas//'dod chags bzhi dang 'bru bcu di rtsa//gnyis med pha rol snang byed pa//rang gi ngang du mnyes byed cing //so so'i mtshan dang dpe byad dag/nges par bstan pa nang gi'o//sems dang sems la bstar ba'i tshogs//
283a4(rnying rgyud): thams cad ye nas dkyil 'khor che//ma bcos rang bzhin lhun gyis grub//lung nas ma lus 'dus pa las//chos gzhan med de yul nyid dang //yul du 'dzin pa snang ba'i bdag/gnyis med rtogs shing khong du chud//mchod bstod rab tu gsang ba yi//lus 
283a5(rnying rgyud): las sprul zhing bsgrubs pas sku//'bru bcu sgra yi 'khor lo gsung //rang grol snying po ma bcos thugs//lha klu gnod sbyin btsan dang bdud//srin po gza' dang mo rigs rnams//dregs shing gdug pa blo zhing gtum//myur mgyogs rmugs shing 'thibs pa dang //
283a6(rnying rgyud): dregs dang dbyings pa so so'i rje//ri rab bshos gtsang rgyan dang bcas//'bru lug snying rtse tsa ru'i dbyibs//dkar dmar bcud ldan dmigs kyis mchod//'a dang rgyal po grogs dang lha//ma dang rje//po la bran lta bu//brten pa rang gi tshul yin no//che btsan 
283a7(rnying rgyud): dbang gi dngos grub dang //longs spyod 'phel zhing mngag par blang //rgyud 'jam skyabs che bka' nyan zhing //drag rtsal ldan zhing slar mi ldog/shin tu nye ba yon tan te//rang la rtsub cing 'tshe ba dang //longs spyod mi 'byor mi 'phrod dang //blo bur na dkyis gzir byed 
283b1(rnying rgyud): cing //slar ldog bkra shis nyams pa dang //bsgo ba mi nyan don med cing //rang rgyud rtsub cing khengs pa dang //rigs rgyud ma smin kun gyis brnyas//bsam don mi grub mang 'thang nyams//rgyud don sbas pa ma shes skyon//khrims kyi bdag po lta bu'i lha//gnad 
283b2(rnying rgyud): kyis brtsing zhing bsgrub par bya//dbang chen pa dang mtshungs pa'i bdud//mtshungs med grogs dang 'dra ba'i btsan//gnod sbyin khrims kyi gtso lta bu//srin po srog la rgol ba'i gshed//gza' rgod lag yan gzhon lta bu//sdong bu dug sbrul lta bu'i klu//
283b3(rnying rgyud): mo rgyud dbang chen rje dpon ma//mtshungs ldan de yi gnyen pos bsgrub//khugs su ma bcug bsgo mi nyan//se long gnad med bdud du 'gyur//bcud phur ma thebs slar la ldog/rang gi rang lo lam bzhar g.yel//lto ba mad krugs las mi myur//
283b4(rnying rgyud): log non ma ldan bdag nyid stob//dbang du ma 'dus bsgo mi nyan//gnad kyi man ngag brgyad kyis ni//mchod bstod bsgrub ste phrin las bcol//zhes bka' stsal to//'jig rten gyi sde dpon chen po rnams kyi zab pa'i mchod bstod gnad kyi le'u ste 
283b5(rnying rgyud): drug pa'o////de nas gzhan yang rab bshad bya//'jig rten dregs pa'i sde dpon rnams//drag po'i las la sbyor ba ni//dur khrod bdug pa'i gnas dag tu//rtsa ba'i bsnyen pa sngon song nas//so so'i mthun pa'i rdzas bsar te//
283b6(rnying rgyud): drag po'i las kyi khang ba ni//e dbyibs sum cu ldan par bsham//phyi nang gsang ba sku gsung thugs//gnas byin brlab dang bskang dang mchod//spyi dang so sor bsngags pa dag/rab tu bde bar 'dod pas dmigs//gzugs sgra bsam pa'i nus pa yis//lus 
283b7(rnying rgyud): dang lus kyi 'phrul 'khor dang //srog dang srog gi gnyen po yis//sems dang sems kyi ngo bo yis//phyi nang kun du bsngags pa ste//'phro 'du 'od kyis drang zhing dgug/rang gzhan dmigs pa de la stim//sha lus khrag sogs ma lus bzas//sregs 
284a1(rnying rgyud): @#//pa'i sde dpon sku gsung thugs//ma lus mnyes pa 'di yis bya//gang la dmigs pa tshad kyis sgrol//ting nge 'dzin gyi brtul zhugs kyis//dul dang myur ba blo yi stobs//'di la the tshom mi bya ste//rdo rje 'chang chen gsang ba'i bka'//
284a2(rnying rgyud): gal te 'bar bas mngon spyod cing //myur du 'pho bar bya ba ni//rno dbal tsha rtsub dri nga ba'i//yam shing gdug pas dbyibs ldan brtsig/gdol ba rigs ngan shan pa dang //g.yung mo grogs thal khyim gyi mes//lbang zhing dug khrag mdar ba la//gzugs 
284a3(rnying rgyud): brnyan rdzogs par longs spyod pa'i//slar yang dmigs te lhag ma dbul//gtso dang 'khor kyi rab phye ba'i//drag po'i tshig gis rab tu bgrang //dregs pa'i bdag po so so yi//che brjod gnad dang bcas pa yis//sta ba cing sbyin pa rim bzhin bya//drang srong phrin 
284a4(rnying rgyud): las rdzogs par bskul//thal chen dang ni drag po'i rdzas//rme khrag gsum dang gsod byed gsum//mtshon bral rin chen gsum gyi dbyig/rang byung bkra shis bzlog pa'i rdzas//nang du 'du 'phror sbyar ba ni//sgra tshig ting nge 'dzin ldan pas//stobs chen 'khor 
284a5(rnying rgyud): los dbab cing 'phang //dregs pa 'khor bcas las su dmigs//gzhan yang mchod rdzas gzugs brnyan byas//rab tu bstan pa'i dmigs pas bstim//'gyu gnas sa dang chu lug dang //sra gsher 'thab dang phung shul dang //rgya mtsho'i dbus deng lam po che//
284a6(rnying rgyud): mtshon gyis brdegs dang rlung gis bskyal//bag med lhung zhing me chus 'joms//lugs nas byung ba'i rten 'brel te//brnag pa'i yid kyis 'dzin phur gdab//gdug byed 'khor dang zas su gtad//ji srid 'khor ba ma stong 'bar//thar bar mi 'gyur las kyi 
284a7(rnying rgyud): gling //byams dang snying rje'i sems ma rtogs//'di ltar mngon par spyad pa yis//bstan la rab sngar phyir gyur pa'i//rjes 'brang log par lta ba rnams//tshar gcod sgrol zhing 'dul ba yi//zhes bka' stsal to//'jig rten gyi sde dpon chen po'i phrin

285b2(rnying rgyud): pa ngag gi brda//gang la brtsams pa de 'grub cing //ma zhum mi spor mi 'dor bas//dbang dang ldan zhing mi g.yo la//gsang ba'i mthu stobs rab tu thob//yid kyi yul du brda 'byung ba'i//snga phyi de ma thag pa'o//bag chags ma yin rmi lam ni//

285b4(rnying rgyud): pa'i tshul 'chang zhing //bka' bsgo las byed nyan pa rtags//ring ba'i gnas nas glu len cing //rgyang mig blta ba g.yel ba'i rtags//rang la chad pa'i las rtsom zhing //nyes lan 'ded pa bzlog pa'i rtags//zas skom 'dod cing 'khrig lan 
285b5(rnying rgyud): bsnyeg/rang nyid gnod 'gyod ma skongs rtags//'dzom zhing dud mos 'debs byed pa//ma dag lus snang nyams pa'i rtags//rang gi bsgo ba mi nyan zhing //slar zhing tshul la 'khol 'dod pa//gnyen pos dbang du ma gyur rtags//mtshon 
285b6(rnying rgyud): cha brdung zhing chod ma ldan//nus med bya ba mi 'grub pas//sngags kyi nus pa ma grub rtags//gzhan 'dod rang la med pa dang //rang gi yod pa 'dod med rnams//mchod bskyang thun rdzas ma tshang rtags//sha rus khrag sogs 
285b7(rnying rgyud): longs spyod cing //ngo mthon rab tu che ba na//las byas 'grub pa'i rtags yin no//pha rol dmag tshogs ma byung zhing //sgo med khang par bdag zhugs la//ngan sngags gzhan gyis bzlog pa'i rtags//bye brag sde brgyad so so ni//mi dang dud 'gro 
286a1(rnying rgyud): @#//'dab chags sogs//pho tshogs nag po bdud kyi rtags//mo tshogs rgyan bzang ma mo ste//che btsan 'bag seng gtsug lag khang //bus ba spra spre rgyal po'i rtags//mi rta khra spyang ri dgas ni//drag btsan ri btsan rtags yin no//
